TL;DR: 本文提出FTF-rl（First Things First Reinforcement Learning）方法，用于提升多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在多优先级用户需求场景下的推理能力。作者构建了3,649个涵盖电商、预订、地图/打车等真实服务场景的推理任务，分三类需求场景评估，发现现有MLLM失败严重，而FTF-rl能显著提升任务成功率，并在LogicVista、MathVision、InfoQA等逻辑数学推理基准上展现泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在满足用户复杂的、结构化的多优先级需求方面表现严重不足，常误解任务要求、违反must-have约束或产生无效解。作者希望填补该研究空白，探索如何让MLLM代理更好地处理"必须满足"与"最好满足"两级需求的推理任务，并学会在无可行解时正确拒答。

Method: 作者构建了3,649个精心构造的问题来覆盖三类需求场景（唯一解、多解优先级排序、无解拒答），评估多个现有MLLM后发现显著缺陷。在此基础上提出FTF-rl（First Things First Reinforcement Learning），通过强化学习显式优化多优先级用户需求上的推理过程。

Result: FTF-rl相比强基线显著提升了任务成功率，同时在LogicVista、MathVision和InfoQA等逻辑数学推理任务上也展现出通用有效性，表明方法具备良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 增强需求感知推理能力（requirement-aware reasoning）是提升MLLM代理泛化能力的一条简单而有效的路径。代码和数据集已开源。

Abstract: Recent progress in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) has fueled significant enthusiasm in their potential to act as autonomous agents for real-world tasks. However, scenarios requiring agents to fulfill users' complex, structured requirements remain largely underexplored. In this work, we examine reasoning tasks under three distinct requirement scenarios: (i) Must-have requirements uniquely determine a unique feasible solution; (ii) Multiple answers satisfy the must-have requirements and are prioritized via the nice-to-have requirements; and (iii) No candidate solution satisfies the must-have requirements, in which case the agent should abstain from generating a response. We evaluate state-of-the-art MLLMs on 3,649 carefully constructed problems that reflect realistic service scenarios, including e-commerce, booking, and map-based or ride-hailing. Our evaluation reveals that existing MLLMs exhibit catastrophic failures in all scenarios. They frequently misinterpret task requirements, violate must-have requirements, and produce invalid solutions. To address this critical gap, we propose First Things First Reinforcement Learning FTF-rl that explicitly optimizes reasoning over multi-priority user requirements. Experimental results show that our method substantially improves the task success rate compared to strong baselines. Moreover, FTF-rl yields general effectiveness on popular logical and mathematical reasoning tasks, including LogicVista, MathVision, and InfoQA. Our findings suggest that enhancing requirement-aware reasoning capability provides a simple yet effective pathway to improve generalization of MLLM agents. Code and dataset are available at https://github.com/claire62/FTF-RL.

</details>


### [67] [Measured Sliders: Learning Continuous Controls from Differentiable Image Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05234)
*Yijia Chen,Boyu Wei,Xuanhua Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Measured Sliders框架，通过闭式可微图像度量来定义连续图像生成控制，统一了观测性测试、度量引导训练、解码校准和LoRA分支组合的完整流程，在SDXL和FLUX.1-dev上实现了有序、选择性和可组合的控制效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型slider的坐标轴来源于文本或学习表征，与可观测的图像属性脱节，导致无法预先判断属性是否可学习、无法直接比较控制强度、也无法预测多控制组合时的干扰。

Method: 1) 用闭式可微图像度量定义连续控制，建立统一的度量空间；2) 训练前通过可观测性测试筛选可用监督；3) 训练中用度量引导目标学习目标移动同时抑制非目标变化；4) 训练后通过解码校准将控制表达为可实现图像变化的可比单位；5) 多个LoRA分支存储在同一checkpoint中，无需联合激活训练即可组合。

Result: 在553个prompt上光照方向达到rho=0.995，98.9%单调扫描。五属性checkpoint平均选择性2.59（最强基线仅1.50），在96.7%的双组合和86.1%的三组合中保留所有请求方向。可观测性测试能区分所有后续成功的度量与失败的候选。

Conclusion: 图像空间的度量学习、诊断、校准和组合连续生成控制提供了共同基础，使扩散模型slider的控制更可预测、可比较和可组合。

Abstract: Continuous sliders are useful only when coefficient changes produce predictable image changes. Yet most diffusion sliders derive their axes from text or learned representations, leaving their scales disconnected from observable image properties. Consequently, we cannot tell in advance which attributes are learnable, compare control strengths directly, or anticipate interference when multiple controls are combined. We propose Measured Sliders, a framework that defines continuous controls through closed-form differentiable image measurements. A common measurement space unifies the pipeline. Before training, an observability test identifies usable supervision. During training, a measurement-guided objective learns target movement while suppressing non-target changes. After training, decoded calibration expresses controls in comparable units of realized image change. Multiple LoRA branches are stored in one checkpoint and composed without training on joint activations. Across SDXL and FLUX.1-dev, the resulting controls are ordered, selective, and composable. On 553 prompts, lighting direction reaches rho = 0.995 and 98.9% monotone sweeps. A five-attribute checkpoint achieves average selectivity 2.59, compared with 1.50 for the strongest baseline, and preserves every requested direction in 96.7% of pair and 86.1% of triple compositions. The observability test also separates every subsequently successful measurement from the failed candidate. Overall, image-space measurement provides a common basis for learning, diagnosing, calibrating, and composing continuous generative controls.

</details>


### [68] [Cross-Domain Tracker Adaptation Without Target-Domain Labels via Vision-Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05239)
*Daniel Davila,Ravikumar Balakrishnan,Mike Cochran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为诊断代理，在无目标域标签的情况下自适应调整检测-跟踪流水线。VLM通过直接检查渲染的跟踪输出、识别视觉失败模式并迭代推荐参数更新。实验表明，在MOT17→MOT20场景中，VLM调优器无需目标域标签即可恢复67.8%因领域迁移丢失的性能裕度；方法在无明确失败模式时拒绝修改配置，从而保持简单迁移的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面真实标签监督的超参数迁移在跨域场景中具有脆弱性，例如MOT17→MOT20中源域最优配置导致HOTA从0.357降至0.267。同时，无标签贝叶斯优化在应对大规模域偏移时表现不佳，甚至可能降低原本良好的配置性能。因此需要一种无需标签的自适应调优方法。

Method: 将VLM作为诊断代理集成到迭代调优循环中：VLM直接检查渲染的跟踪输出（而非优化标注指标），识别视觉失败模式，据此推荐检测-跟踪流水线的参数更新；当视觉诊断未发现明确失败模式时，系统拒绝修改配置以保持性能。

Result: 在MOT17→MOT20迁移中，VLM调优器恢复67.8%的丢失性能空间，与目标域上限仅差0.029 HOTA；在最高密度目标序列上恢复高达86.7%。相比之下，无标签贝叶斯优化在大域偏移下表现不佳。VLM调优器在简单迁移上保持性能，在困难迁移上改善性能。该方法在域偏移通过暴露的检测级参数显现时有效，但在MOT17→DanceTrack等源域最优配置已接近最优的场景中效果有限。

Conclusion: VLM作为无需标签的诊断代理进行迭代调优，为跨域跟踪系统自适应提供了一种有效途径，尤其适用于较大域偏移且错误暴露于检测级参数的情况。同时，该方法的"选择性"行为——仅在识别到明确失败模式时才修改配置——是其优于无标签贝叶斯优化的关键设计特性。

Abstract: We present a system that uses a Vision-Language Model (VLM) as a diagnostic agent for adapting a detect-to-track pipeline to a new target domain without access to target-domain labels. Rather than optimizing against annotated metrics, the VLM directly inspects rendered tracking outputs, identifies visual failure modes, and recommends parameter updates through an iterative tuning loop. We first demonstrate that ground-truth-supervised hyperparameter transfer can be brittle. On MOT17->MOT20, applying a source-derived oracle configuration reduces mean HOTA by 0.090, from a target-domain ceiling of 0.357, to 0.267. Without using any target-domain labels, our VLM-based tuner recovers 67.8% of this lost headroom, finishing within 0.029 HOTA of the target ceiling; on the highest-density target sequence, it recovers up to 86.7%. We further show that label-free Bayesian optimization with handcrafted proxy objectives struggles under large domain shifts and can degrade configurations that are already strong. In contrast, the VLM tuner acts selectively: when its visual diagnosis reveals no clear failure mode, it declines to modify the configuration, preserving performance on easy transfers while improving hard ones. Finally, we characterize the conditions under which this approach succeeds, namely, when domain shift manifests through exposed detection-level parameters, versus where it is less effective, such as MOT17->DanceTrack, where the source oracle is already near-optimal.

</details>


### [69] [Few-Shot Video Recognition via Hierarchical Metric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05242)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Haoran Gao,Xizhan Gao,Zihao Dong,Tingwei Wang,Sijie Niu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HML-FSAR方法，通过空间增强模块捕获跨帧全局空间信息，并嵌入分层度量学习策略，在五个FSAR基准数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本动作识别方法通常仅在网络输出层施加单一原型监督，未能充分利用视频中丰富的跨帧全局空间信息；即使有方法引入多层级度量方案，也只是对中间层施加并行的原型约束，缺乏沿完整特征管道的渐进式监督，导致学到的类原型泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出HML-FSAR方法：1）构建空间增强模块以捕获跨帧全局空间表示，并与时间MHA、异质对齐、时空特征融合和字典学习模块共同构成完整特征处理管道；2）嵌入分层度量学习（HML）策略，由中心度量、对齐度量、对比度量、字典度量和原型度量组成，从帧级表示到最终类原型施加渐进式多阶段互补约束，联合优化特征紧凑性、异质时空对齐、类间判别性和抗噪鲁棒性。

Result: 在五个广泛使用的少样本动作识别数据集上进行了验证，实验结果充分证明了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: HML-FSAR通过分层渐进式度量学习替代单一原型监督，能够更好地利用跨帧全局空间信息并提升类原型的泛化能力，在FSAR任务上取得了优越性能。

Abstract: Few-shot action recognition (FSAR) aims to recognize unseen action categories with only a small number of annotated video samples. Recent works typically apply single-prototype supervision at the network output and fail to sufficiently exploit rich cross-frame global spatial information in videos. Even existing multi-level metric schemes only impose parallel prototype constraints on intermediate layers, without progressive supervision along the full feature pipeline, which results in limited generalization ability of the learned class prototypes. Inspired by this, we present a novel method, hierarchical metric learning for few-shot action recognition (HML-FSAR). First, a spatial-enhanced module is developed to capture cross-frame global spatial representations. Combined with temporal MHA, heterogeneous alignment, spatial-temporal feature fusion and dictionary learning modules, it constructs the complete feature processing pipeline. Second, a hierarchical metric learning (HML) strategy is embedded into HML-FSAR. Composed of center metric, alignment metric, contrastive metric, dictionary metric and prototype metric, HML imposes progressive multi-stage complementary constraints from frame-level representations to final class prototypes, so as to jointly optimize feature compactness, heterogeneous spatial-temporal alignment, inter-class discriminability and anti-noise robustness. The proposed HML-FSAR method is validated on five widely-used FSAR datasets, and experimental results fully demonstrate its effectiveness.

</details>


### [70] [Compact Neural Appearance Models for Efficient Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05255)
*Florian Hahlbohm,Jorge Condor,Linus Franke,Martin Eisemann,Marcus Magnor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对3D高斯泼溅中基于球谐函数（SH）的外观建模与多种新型球形外观模型进行了端到端对比，并提出了一种基于紧凑逐基元隐代码和共享小型MLP的隐式神经外观表示。该表示将逐基元外观占用从192字节降至28字节（相较于三阶SH），优化速度提升1.3倍，同时改善重建质量。作者在统一的可微CUDA光栅化流水线中集成所有模型，并提供便携式WebGL查看器，从重建质量、内存占用和性能等维度进行全面评估，还分析了外观参数化对优化过程、几何重建及非静态场景内容吸收的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯泼溅等显式基元辐射场通常用低阶球谐函数建模视角相关外观，但SH系数占据大量逐基元存储与内存流量，且带限基限制了角度细节表达。现有SH的高效性与新型球形外观模型的表达能力之间缺乏系统性的公平对比，需要更紧凑且表达能力更强的替代方案。

Method: 提出一种隐式神经外观模型，将紧凑的逐基元隐代码通过共享的小型MLP解码为外观表示。将所有模型（SH与多种球形模型）集成到同一优化的可微CUDA光栅化管线和便携式WebGL查看器中，实现融合前向与反向传播的公平端到端比较。

Result: 近期提出的球形外观模型在质量-效率权衡方面表现最佳；所提出的神经表示是最紧凑的模型，相比三阶SH将逐基元外观足迹从192字节降至28字节，优化速度提升1.3倍并改善重建质量。分析还揭示了不同外观参数化在优化行为、几何恢复及对非静态场景内容吸收能力上的差异。

Conclusion: 本文框架与分析为超越SH的外观建模提供了超越图像指标层面的实用指导，表明更紧凑的隐式神经表示在保持甚至提升重建质量的同时能显著降低内存和计算开销。

Abstract: Explicit primitive-based radiance fields such as 3D Gaussian Splatting typically model view-dependent appearance using low-order spherical harmonics (SH). Although efficient to evaluate, SH coefficients dominate per-primitive storage and memory traffic, while their band-limited basis restricts angular detail. We present a thorough, end-to-end comparison of SH and recent spherical appearance models and introduce an implicit alternative that decodes compact per-primitive latent codes using a tiny shared MLP. We integrate all models into the same optimized pipeline, fusing their forward and backward passes into a differentiable CUDA rasterizer and provide a portable WebGL viewer for laptop and mobile GPUs. Our evaluation across reconstruction quality, memory use, and optimization and rendering performance shows that recent spherical models offer the strongest overall quality-efficiency trade-off. Our neural representation is the most compact model evaluated and, compared to third-degree SH, reduces the per-primitive appearance footprint from 192 to 28 bytes, accelerates optimization by 1.3$\times$, while improving reconstruction quality. We further analyze how appearance parametrization shapes optimization, identifying differences in recovered geometry and the tendency of expressive models to absorb non-static scene content. Together, our framework and analysis provide practical guidance for replacing SH beyond what image metrics alone can capture.

</details>


### [71] [Learning Spatial-Spectral Refinement and Calibrating Complementary Observations for Hyperspectral Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05303)
*Liqian Yang,Xingchi Chen,Xinfeng Gui,Xiangyong Cao,Qianxin Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TSR-ITNR，一种两阶段自监督框架，将隐式张量神经表示与观测引导校准相结合，用于高光谱与多光谱图像融合，无需真值高分辨率高光谱图像监督即可实现高质量重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于隐式神经表示（INR）的HMIF方法未能充分捕捉细粒度空间结构和丰富的谱间依赖关系，且低分辨率高光谱图像（LR-HSI）与高分辨率多光谱图像（HR-MSI）主要通过退化一致性约束融合，互补信息利用不足。

Method: TSR-ITNR包含两个阶段：第一阶段学习隐式Tucker表示，通过优化低秩空间系数张量和谱基以捕捉细粒度空间结构与波段间相关性，并利用固定预训练去噪器提供深层先验；第二阶段通过无参数校准从两种观测中提取互补且互不干扰的修正信息，以恢复第一阶段遗漏的信息。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在定量指标、视觉质量和光谱重建方面均表现出强性能，且无需真值HR-HSI监督；下游语义分割精度进一步验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: 理论分析证明了谱精化的几何保持性质和校准的正交互补性，TSR-ITNR在无监督条件下实现了性能优越且具有可解释性的高光谱图像重建。

Abstract: Hyperspectral and multispectral image fusion (HMIF) aims to reconstruct a high-resolution hyperspectral image (HR-HSI) by combining the fine spatial details of a high-resolution multispectral image (HR-MSI) with the rich spectral information of a low-resolution hyperspectral image (LR-HSI). Recent advances in implicit neural representations (INRs) have enabled flexible coordinate-based modeling for HMIF; however, existing INR-based approaches may not fully capture fine-grained spatial structures and rich spectral dependencies. Moreover, the LR-HSI and HR-MSI are primarily incorporated through degradation-consistency constraints, leaving their complementary information underexploited. To address these limitations, we propose Two-Stage Reconstruction with Implicit Tensor Neural Representation (TSR-ITNR), a unified self-supervised framework integrating representation refinement and observation-guided calibration. In Stage 1, TSR-ITNR learns an implicit Tucker representation and refines its low-rank spatial coefficient tensor and spectral basis to better capture fine spatial structures and interband correlations. A fixed pretrained denoiser further provides a deep prior for the preliminary reconstruction. In Stage 2, parameter-free calibration derives complementary and noninterfering corrections from both observations to recover information insufficiently captured in Stage 1. Theoretical analysis establishes the geometry-preserving property of spectral refinement and the orthogonal complementarity of calibration. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate strong quantitative, visual, and spectral reconstruction performance without ground-truth HR-HSI supervision. Beyond conventional reconstruction metrics, we further assess the effectiveness of TSR-ITNR using downstream semantic segmentation accuracy.

</details>


### [72] [Adaptive Gated Deepfake Detection for Low-Resolution and Resource-Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05320)
*Vaishnavi Sen,Cody Laurie,Rashida Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaGate-DF，一种自适应门控深度伪造检测框架，利用图像质量线索通过双多出口系统路由样本，使高质量图像能提前退出以节省计算资源，在Celeb-DF和FaceForensics++数据集上实现了检测性能与计算效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测模型依赖高质量输入、固定推理路径和高计算开销架构，限制了其在低分辨率和资源受限场景中的实际应用。

Method: 提出AdaGate-DF自适应门控框架，利用图像质量线索将样本路由到双多出口系统，高质量图像可更早退出推理流程以节省计算成本。

Result: 在Celeb-DF上AUC达0.9370，优于MaD-CoRN和DefakeHop++且保持低推理延迟；384×384分辨率下AUC提升至0.9708；FaceForensics++上在类别不平衡条件下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: AdaGate-DF在检测性能、不确定性感知预测与计算效率之间实现了实际可行的平衡，适用于可变质量的深度伪造检测场景。

Abstract: Deepfake detection models often rely on high-quality inputs, fixed inference paths, and computationally expensive architectures, limiting their use in low-resolution and resource-constrained settings. This paper proposes AdaGate-DF, an adaptive gated deepfake detection framework that uses image-quality cues to route samples through a dual multi-exit system so high-quality images can exit earlier and save compute. We evaluated AdaGate-DF against MaD-CoRN, DefakeHop++, and ShuffleNetV2 on two benchmark datasets (Celeb-DF and FaceForensics++) under multiple configurations to test image resolution dependence and training and inference efficiency. On Celeb-DF, AdaGate-DF achieves an AUC of 0.9370, outperforming MaD-CoRN and DefakeHop++ while maintaining a low inference latency. Resolution-based testing shows consistent improvement as input resolution increases, reaching an AUC of 0.9708 at 384 by 384. The FaceForensics++ results highlight that AdaGate-DF remains effective under class imbalance, following competitive results with evaluated models. Overall, AdaGate-DF demonstrated a practical balance between detection performance, uncertainty-aware prediction, and computational efficiency for variable-quality deepfake detection.

</details>


### [73] [Scalable Detection of Fossil Palynomorphs in Multifocal Digital Microscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05323)
*Abbas Shaikh,Praise Mayor,Patrick Ainlay-Vazquez,Aditya Viswanathan,Teon Golden,Eric Zhang,Ingrid C. Romero,Alexander E. White,Scott Wing,Arko Barman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个可扩展的端到端自动化孢粉体检测流程，通过图像分解压缩、目标检测模型基准测试、跨大尺度图像检测输出合成算法和I/O优化，将单个载玻片的孢粉体检测时间从数天缩短至1小时以内，AP@50达0.879。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孢粉体（如花粉、孢子、甲藻囊）是重建古气候和研究古生态系统的重要高分辨率记录，但现有方法依赖人工分析高分辨率、多焦点数字显微镜图像，耗时巨大且限制了研究的规模。

Method: 提出了四部分组成的端到端自动化管线：(1) 将数字化多焦点载玻片图像分解并压缩为可处理的二维图像的效率方法；(2) 对现代目标检测模型（包括RF-DETR）进行孢粉体检测基准测试；(3) 跨大规模高分辨率图像合成检测输出的高效算法；(4) I/O优化以加速推理。

Result: 在孢粉体检测中实现AP@50达0.879的性能，并将单个载玻片的检测时间从数天的人工检查缩短至不到一小时的自动化分析。

Conclusion: 该方法大幅降低了孢粉体检测所需时间，使得更大规模的孢粉学研究成为可能，为古气候与古生态系统研究提供了高效的自动化工具。

Abstract: Palynomorphs (microscopic, organic-walled fossils such as pollen, spores, and dinoflagellates) are important high-resolution records of past climates and are critical to the study of ancient ecosystems. Existing methods rely on manual analysis of high-resolution, multifocal digital microscopy images, which is slow and time-consuming and requires researchers to compromise on the scale of their investigations. To the best of our knowledge, our work proposes the first ever scalable end-to-end pipeline for automated palynomorph detection in whole slide images that addresses this bottleneck through: (1) efficient methods for decomposing and compressing digitized multifocal microscope slide images into tractable 2-dimensional tiles for analysis; (2) benchmarking modern object detection models, including RF-DETR, for the detection of palynomorphs, achieving an AP@50 of 0.879; (3) an efficient algorithm for the synthesis of detection outputs across large-scale, high-resolution images; and (4) an I/O optimization resulting in faster inference time. Our methods drastically reduce the time required for palynomorph detection in a single slide from often days of manual inspection to under one hour of automated analysis, enabling palynological research at a substantially greater scale.

</details>


### [74] [Lightweight Vision Transformer Compression for On-Device Plant Disease Detection in Resource-Constrained Agricultural Field Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05334)
*Mahadev Sunil Kumar,Bhavika Gondi,Desaisetty Venkata Satya Sai Swapnith,Gangireddy Rahul Jogi,Sudheesh Manalil,Arnab Raha,Amitava Mukherjee,Parthasarathy Seethapathy,G. Gopakumar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的自注意力压缩框架，将基于二阶敏感性估计的Hessian平衡自适应块剪枝（H-BAC）、量化与基于注意力的知识蒸馏相结合，应用于辣椒病害分类任务。在真实跨村庄分布外测试集上，压缩后模型在保持95.13%基线精度的同时实现了最高54.5x的尺寸缩减（327.42MB降至6.01MB），但直接训练的同等尺寸学生模型也能达到94.87%的精度，提示了H-BAC与知识蒸馏的相对收益尚需进一步验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 辣椒病害识别对印度农业至关重要，但ViT模型计算开销大难以部署于资源受限设备。现有压缩方法（剪枝、量化、知识蒸馏）通常被孤立使用，其组合应用的效果与相互作用未被充分探索，需要统一的压缩框架以兼顾精度与效率。

Method: 提出统一ViT压缩框架，核心组件包括：(1)基于Hessian二阶灵敏度引导的H-BAC自适应块剪枝；(2)量化；(3)基于注意力的知识蒸馏。先对各技术进行独立消融实验选出最优配置，再将其集成到面向农业实际约束的顺序部署流水线中。

Result: 在辣椒3类村庄分割数据集上（含真实跨村庄、跨设备OOD测试集），压缩模型匹配或超越95.13% FP32基线精度，实现74-98%模型尺寸缩减；完整压缩流水线以95.13±2.32%精度实现54.5x尺寸缩减（327.42MB→6.01MB）。直接训练的同等尺寸学生模型（6.01MB INT8）达94.87%精度，与压缩方案相当。

Conclusion: 统一压缩框架能显著减小ViT部署规模且保持精度，但直接训练小模型同样能达到可比精度，表明H-BAC和知识蒸馏在本数据集上的计算成本是否值得仍需进一步研究，未来需在更大规模、更复杂的数据集上验证其优势。

Abstract: Chilli (Capsicum annuum) is one of India's most economically significant crops, yet its productivity is persistently threatened by diseases that are difficult to identify without expert intervention. While Vision Transformers (ViTs) have achieved high classification accuracy, their large computational footprint makes deployment on resource constrained devices challenging. Existing compression approaches typically address pruning, quantization, and knowledge distillation in isolation, leaving the potential benefits and interactions of their combined application insufficiently explored. We propose a unified Vision Transformer compression framework that combines Hessian-Balanced Adaptive Block Pruning (H-BAC), guided by second-order sensitivity estimation, with quantization and attention-based knowledge distillation. To systematically identify the most effective configuration within each compression family, each technique is first evaluated independently through controlled ablation studies, after which the best-performing components are integrated into a sequential deployment pipeline tailored to real-world agricultural constraints. On a chilli 3-class village-split dataset with a genuine cross-village, cross-device out-of-distribution test split, the resulting compressed models match or exceed the 95.13% FP32 baseline's accuracy, alongside 74-98% model size reduction, and the fully integrated compression pipeline achieves a 54.5x size reduction (327.42 MB to 6.01 MB) at 95.13 +/- 2.32% accuracy across four tested configurations. A direct comparison further reveals that, on this dataset, a directly-trained student of the same final size, without pruning or distillation, reaches comparable accuracy of 94.87%, at the same 6.01 MB INT8 size, indicating where H-BAC and knowledge distillation are, and are not yet shown to be, worth their computational cost.

</details>


### [75] [MEOX: Compact Multimodal Mixture-of-Experts for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05351)
*Mohanad Albughdadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MEOX是一个紧凑型多模态掩码自编码器（总共仅311.5万参数），通过传感器特定适配器、显式有效性信号和共享稀疏专家块实现异构传感器与缺失观测的自适应表征学习，在GEO-Bench六项任务上表现优异，并在参数预算极小的情况下超越CSMoE相关结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测表征学习方法通常依赖更大规模的架构来处理异构传感器和缺失观测数据，这带来了较高的计算与存储成本，研究希望探索在紧凑参数预算下实现传感器灵活表征学习与强任务迁移的可能性。

Method: 提出MEOX多模态掩码自编码器：包含传感器特定适配器、显式有效性信号、共享稀疏专家块、学习式patch级融合，四个元数据token伴随空间序列经过编码器模块；共享专家投影配合私有低秩残差约束参数增长，旋转注意力支持不同空间网格；在122.8万MMEarth64样本上以模态均衡掩码重建和结构化传感器丢弃进行预训练。

Result: 在GEO-Bench六项任务上：cashew分割64像素达64.42%平均IoU，EuroSAT在224像素达90.56%平均准确率，均超过CSMoE报告结果；BigEarthNet微调达72.95%微平均精度；WorldCover探测显示元数据带来0.64个百分点增益；路由诊断能区分专家参与度、空间依赖、模态关联和功能贡献。

Conclusion: MEOX证明在极小参数预算下依然可以实现传感器灵活的表示学习与强任务迁移能力，其紧凑架构设计和有效的路由机制为地球观测多模态学习提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent advances in Earth Observation representation learning accommodate heterogeneous sensors and missing observations, often through larger architectures. We present MEOX (Multimodal Earth Observation with eXperts), a multimodal masked autoencoder with a 2.939 million-parameter encoder and 3.115 million parameters in total. Sensor-specific adapters, explicit validity signals, and a shared sparse-expert block preserve modality-dependent processing before a learned patch-wise fusion. Four metadata tokens then accompany a single spatial sequence through fourteen further encoder blocks. Shared expert projections with private low-rank residuals constrain parameter growth, while rotary attention supports downstream spatial grids different from pretraining. The model is pretrained on 1.228 million MMEarth64 samples using modality-balanced masked reconstruction and structured sensor dropout. Frozen transfer is evaluated on six GEO-Bench tasks at both 64 and 224 pixels. The model reaches 64.42% mean intersection-over-union on cashew segmentation at 64 pixels and 90.56% average accuracy on EuroSAT at 224 pixels, exceeding the corresponding reported CSMoE results. BigEarthNet finetuning reaches 72.95% micro-average precision. Routing diagnostics distinguish expert participation, spatial dependence, modality association, and functional contribution. A held-out WorldCover probe measures a 0.64-percentage-point benefit from metadata, while retrieval separates same-sensor semantics from cross-sensor alignment. These results demonstrate sensor-flexible representation learning and strong task transfer using a compact parameter budget.

</details>


### [76] [Reflection-aware Generative Novel View Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05382)
*GeonU Kim,Shin Dong-Yeon,Tae-Hyun Oh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ref-GeNVS，一种无需训练、具有反射感知能力的生成式新视角合成（NVS）方法，专门用于镜子场景。核心思想是将镜面图像视为两个互补视图，通过估计镜面平面、反射相机位姿构建虚拟视图，并采用两阶段生成方法（Mirror-gated attention和Reflection injection），在无需微调的情况下实现反射一致的NVS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多视角扩散模型无法识别场景中的镜子，也无法利用反射内容进行场景生成，导致在镜子场景中生成的新视角缺乏反射一致性。

Method: 1) 从输入图像估计镜面平面，反射相机位姿形成虚拟视图；2) 将镜面图像视为两个互补视图；3) 提出两阶段生成方法：Mirror-gated attention（镜面门控注意力）和Reflection injection（反射注入），在多视角扩散模型中显式利用反射关系。整个过程无需额外训练或微调。

Result: 在包含镜子的合成场景和真实场景上，Ref-GeNVS在生成反射一致且上下文连贯的新视图方面优于近期生成式NVS方法，能够揭示仅通过镜子可见的场景结构。

Conclusion: Ref-GeNVS通过将镜面反射建模为虚拟视图中显式的几何关系，继承了多视角扩散模型强大的泛化能力的同时，无需微调即可实现反射感知的生成式新视角合成。

Abstract: We propose Ref-GeNVS, a training-free, reflection-aware method for generative novel view synthesis (NVS) in mirror scenes. Existing multi-view diffusion models often fail to recognize the mirror in the scene and cannot exploit reflected content for scene generation. To fix this issue without additional training, our key idea is to treat a mirror image as two complementary views. From input images, we estimate the mirror plane and reflect camera poses to form virtual views. Based on this virtual view setup, we propose a two-stage generation method consisting of Mirror-gated attention and Reflection injection, which enables reflection-consistent NVS by explicitly leveraging reflection relationships in a multi-view diffusion model. Ref-GeNVS inherits the strong generalizability of the multi-view diffusion backbone, while it does not require finetuning. On synthetic and real scenes including mirrors, Ref-GeNVS outperforms recent generative NVS methods by generating reflection-consistent and contextually coherent novel views, revealing scene structure visible only through mirrors. Project page: https://kim-geonu.github.io/Ref-GeNVS/

</details>


### [77] [Think-Verify-Revise: Neuro-Symbolic Visual Reasoning with Vision-Language Models and Dynamic Logic Tensor Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05388)
*Homayoun Afshari,Pietro Basci,Alessandro Russo,Lia Morra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个神经符号（NeSy）框架，通过将视觉语言模型（VLM）用于自动一阶逻辑规则归纳，与动态逻辑张量网络（D-LTN）用于可微规则验证相结合，形成闭环迭代反馈机制，在ViSudo-PC基准的四个视觉域上仅用三个训练样例即可诱导出有效的数独约束规则，AUC指标达到或超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉推理任务需要同时感知视觉内容并应用形式化的关系约束，纯神经网络和纯符号方法在单独处理时均表现不佳，现有方法无法有效弥合两者之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出Neuro-Symbolic（NeSy）框架，具体包含三个阶段：（1）Think：VLM接收少量标注视觉样例，依据严格语法提出候选FOL规则；（2）Verify：D-LTN在运行时由规则自动组装，基于CNN产生的视觉embedding进行可微规则验证；（3）Revise：将验证失败信息反馈给VLM以引导下一轮假设修正，形成闭环迭代。

Result: 在ViSudo-PC基准的四个视觉域（MNIST、EMNIST、KMNIST、FMNIST）上，系统仅使用三个训练样例即可诱导出有效的Sudoku约束规则，AUC指标匹配或超越了NeuPSL和LTN等前方法。

Conclusion: 实验表明通过VLM进行自动规则发现具有可行性，神经符号框架能有效结合视觉感知与符号推理的优势，代码已开源供进一步研究。

Abstract: Visual reasoning tasks require a system to jointly perceive visual content and apply formal relational constraints---a combination that neither pure neural nor purely symbolic approaches handle well in isolation. This paper proposes a Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) framework that closes this gap by tightly coupling a Vision-Language Model (VLM) for automatic First-Order Logic (FOL) rule induction with a Dynamic Logic Tensor Network (D-LTN) for differentiable rule verification, in a closed iterative feedback loop. The VLM receives a small set of labelled visual examples and proposes candidate FOL rules conforming to a strict grammar (Think); the D-LTN is automatically assembled from these rules at runtime and evaluates them grounding on CNN-produced visual embeddings (Verify); and verification failures are fed back to guide the VLM's next hypothesis (Revise). Evaluated on the ViSudo-PC benchmark across four visual domains (MNIST, EMNIST, KMNIST, FMNIST), the system induces valid Sudoku constraint rules using only three training examples as visual context. The proposed method achieves AUC scores matching or outperforming previous methods (NeuPSL, LTN), showing the potential for automatic rule discovery through VLM. Code is available at https://github.com/homayoun-afshari/nesy.

</details>


### [78] [From Interpretability Methods to Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05399)
*Julien Colin,Nuria Oliver,Thomas Serre*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文主张XAI（可解释AI）领域应从以方法为中心转向以模型为中心，利用现有成熟的解释工具来表征和比较不同模型的可解释性，并提出了一个模型中心的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI领域经过十余年发展已拥有成熟的工具集，但绝大多数研究精力都花在构建和比较解释方法上，却忽略了这些方法本应回答的核心问题——模型本身的可解释性如何，以及随着模型演进我们是否在进步。作者认为需要将领域焦点从方法转向模型。

Method: 作者通过综述现有XAI工具箱的成熟度，梳理了少量已有的模型比较研究，并与系统神经科学进行类比，论证了以模型为中心的XAI研究路径的可行性，最后提出模型中心的研究议程。

Result: 论文提出了两条互补的研究路线：一是利用现有工具表征和比较不同模型所代表和计算的内容（已在可及范围内）；二是衡量模型能否真正被依赖它的人类（独立评估者，而非确认预期专家）所理解——这只能被测量而非推断，是目前被忽视的更难方向。

Conclusion: XAI工具已足够成熟以支撑模型中心的研究转型，作者呼吁将评估主体从方法构建者转向现实中的独立人类评估者，推动模型可理解性的实证测量。

Abstract: More than a decade in, explainable AI (XAI) for computer vision has assembled a mature toolbox: attribution, feature visualization, concept-based, and circuit-based methods. Yet almost all of the field's effort has gone into building and comparing these methods, and little into the question they were meant to answer---how interpretable are our models, and are we making progress as they evolve? We argue for shifting the field's focus from methods to models, along two complementary lines. One is already within reach: existing tools let us characterize and compare what different models represent and compute. The other is harder, and largely neglected: whether a model can actually be understood by the humans who rely on it---the independent evaluators on whom trust and certification depend, not the experts confirming what they already expect. It can only be measured, not inferred. We review why the toolbox is mature enough to support both, survey the thin body of work comparing models, draw a parallel to systems neuroscience, and close with a model-centric XAI agenda.

</details>


### [79] [A Generalizable Feature Extractor for Alzheimer's-Related Brain MRI Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05400)
*Reza Rajabli,D. Louis Collins*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了一个用于脑龄预测的紧凑3D CNN预训练模型是否可作为可复用的基础模型。通过冻结其718万参数并仅使用约1%的可训练参数（LoRA适配），在六个神经影像学任务中验证了其泛化能力，包括AD分类、跨数据集迁移、MCI进展预测、淀粉样蛋白阳性预测以及脑区体积估计，均取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在神经影像学领域，尤其是阿尔茨海默病研究中，标注数据通常不足难以训练深度模型。作者希望探究迁移学习在此场景下的有效性，并验证紧凑的预训练模型是否能在不同任务和数据集之间复用而不需要为每个特定任务重新训练。

Method: 作者冻结了一个先前用于脑龄预测的3D CNN的全部7.18M权重作为骨干模型，使用Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA)仅增加约1%的可训练参数来适配不同下游任务。通过六个实验评估模型的泛化能力，包括同数据集分类、跨数据集零训练迁移、MCI进展预测、淀粉样蛋白预测及结构MRI体积回归任务。

Result: 六个实验均取得良好表现：ADNI上痴呆分类AUC达0.964；模型未重训直接应用于OASIS-3得到AUC 0.871；MCI稳定与进展区分AUC 0.828；淀粉样蛋白阳性预测AUC 0.804；海马体和白质高信号体积估计R²分别为0.80和0.91，后者通常需要更大的U-Net网络。

Conclusion: 一个在脑龄预测上训练的紧凑模型可以在仅增加约1%参数的情况下充当多任务可复用骨干，并能无需训练直接迁移到未见过的队列。该研究表明，精心训练的脑龄模型在数据受限条件下可作为阿尔茨海默病相关任务的有效基础模型。

Abstract: When there is not enough labeled data to properly train deep learning models, transfer learning can help. We still do not fully understand how effective it is in neuroimaging, especially for Alzheimer's disease research. It is also not clear if these transferred models can work on new datasets without being retrained for each specific task. We evaluate whether a compact, supervised pretrained model can serve as a reusable foundation model for downstream neuroimaging tasks. We freeze the 7.18 million weights of a 3D CNN previously trained for brain-age prediction, and adapt it to each task using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), requiring only ~1% additional trainable parameters. We evaluate generalizability in six experiments. Adapting the model to classify cognitively normal versus Dementia on ADNI gave an AUC of 0.964 on held-out folds (Experiment #1). Applying that adapted model unchanged to OASIS-3, with no retraining, gave an AUC of 0.871 (Experiment #2). Reusing its output logit together with age and a cognitive score distinguished stable from progressing MCI with an AUC of 0.828 (Experiment #3). Adapting the same backbone to predict amyloid positivity from structural MRI gave an AUC of 0.804 (Experiment #4). Finally, the same approach estimated ICV-normalized hippocampal and white matter hypointensity volumes directly from the T1w image, with R^2 of 0.80 and 0.91 respectively, tasks normally addressed with much larger U-Net networks (Experiments #5 and #6). A compact model supervised on brain age can therefore serve as a reusable backbone, adapting to each task with ~1% additional parameters and transferring to an unseen cohort without any training. Our findings suggest that a carefully trained brain age model can serve as an effective foundation model for Alzheimer's related tasks, even under strict data constraints.

</details>


### [80] [UniMate: One Unified Model to Animate Diverse Skeletons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05415)
*Linzhan Mou,Jiahui Lei,Zhiyang Dou,Chenyue Cai,Chaoyue Song,Adam Finkelstein,Szymon Rusinkiewicz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniMate是一个统一的基础模型，能从绑定的3D资产和文本提示中为任意骨骼合成关节运动，无需测试时优化或按骨骼重新训练。它通过拓扑感知的扩散Transformer（结合图感知注意力偏置、频谱旋转位置嵌入和全局拓扑条件器）以及新构建的UniML3D数据集（含13,006条多类别运动序列），在质量、泛化和效率上超越现有基线，支持零样本跨拓扑迁移、补间、扩展和文本引导编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动绑骨技术已能大规模生成动画就绪的3D资产，但驱动这些资产的运动生成仍是瓶颈。已有的学习方法受拓扑限制，依赖类别特定模板或需要按骨骼微调和推理时参考运动，缺乏对任意骨骼的泛化能力。

Method: 提出拓扑感知的扩散Transformer（UniMate），通过三种机制将骨骼拓扑整合到注意力中：1）基于成对关节关系和测地距离的图感知注意力偏置；2）通过图拉普拉斯将RoPE推广到任意运动树的频谱旋转位置嵌入；3）从静态姿态骨骼注意力池化而来的全局拓扑条件器。同时构建UniML3D数据集，包含跨双足、四足、鸟类、海洋、昆虫、蛇形和关节刚性物体的13,006条运动序列，并进行统一规范化和文本配对。

Result: 在质量、泛化和效率方面均优于现有最先进基线，并支持零样本跨拓扑迁移、补间（in-betweening）、扩展和文本引导编辑。

Conclusion: UniMate作为统一骨骼动画生成基础模型，打破了拓扑约束，展示了对任意骨骼结构和多样运动类别的零样本泛化能力，为大规模自动化动画生成提供了通用解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in automatic rigging now deliver animation-ready 3D assets at scale, yet generating the motion to drive them remains a bottleneck. Existing learned animators are topology-constrained: they rely on category-specific templates or require per-skeleton fine-tuning and reference motions at inference. We present UniMate, a unified foundation model that synthesizes articulated motion for arbitrary skeletons from a rigged 3D asset and a text prompt, with no test-time optimization or per-skeleton retraining. UniMate introduces a topology-aware diffusion transformer, which integrates skeletal topology into attention via three mechanisms: (1) a graph-aware attention bias from pairwise joint relations and geodesic distances; (2) a spectral rotary position embedding generalizing RoPE to arbitrary kinematic trees via the graph Laplacian; and (3) a global topological conditioner attention-pooled from the rest-pose skeleton. We also curate UniML3D, 13,006 motion sequences spanning bipedal, quadrupedal, avian, marine, insectoid, serpentine, and articulated rigid objects with unified canonicalization and text pairing. Trained on this dataset, UniMate outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in quality, generalization, and efficiency, and supports zero-shot cross-topology transfer, in-betweening, expansion, and text-guided editing. Our project page is available at https://linzhanmou.com/unimate/.

</details>


### [81] [WorldSculpt: Generating Compositional Worlds from Grounded Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05416)
*Muyao Niu,Jixuan He,Ruihan Yu,Lian Fu,Yonghao Yu,Zheng-Hui Huang,Yifan Zhan,Fengbo Lan,Yongtao Ge,Yinqiang Zheng,Kaipeng Zhang,Zhixiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过适配单物体3D生成先验来组合式生成包含数百个物体的杂乱3D场景的方法，无需场景级训练即可推广到大场景，并引入了UE-MeshyScene基准数据集用于评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理密集杂乱场景时面临挑战：基于几何的方法通常将场景重建为单一表示，在遮挡区域留下不完整几何；而现有的组合式方法依赖生成先验，但大多局限于较简单的场景。实际应用（如游戏、AR/VR、仿真和机器人）需要将场景表示为共享世界坐标系中的独立物体网格集合，因此需要一种能够处理数百个物体且严重遮挡场景的组合式生成方法。

Method: 提出一种组合式生成范式：将强单物体3D生成先验适应到多视角观测中。具体以Pixal3D为实例化基础，扩展了一个多视角条件通路，使物体生成基于多个带姿态的观测。模型仅使用单物体在规范空间中进行微调，无需场景级训练即可推广到大量遮挡的复杂场景。此外，引入了UE-MeshyScene基准，包含数百个物体的密集杂乱场景，带有逐物体标注和ground-truth网格。

Result: 在单物体、受控多物体和UE-MeshyScene评估中，该方法一致优于先前方法，并且随着场景复杂度和遮挡程度的增加，性能提升更为显著。还展示了将生成的3DGS世界（如Marble和HY-World 2.0）转换为组合式网格场景的更广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明通过适应强单物体3D生成先验，可以无需场景级训练即可组合式生成包含数百个物体、严重遮挡的复杂场景，验证了这一范式的可行性和可扩展性，并提供了公开基准和一致的性能优势。

Abstract: We study the problem of generating a compositional 3D representation of a cluttered scene containing hundreds of objects. The goal is to represent the scene as a collection of individual object meshes placed in a shared world frame, as required by downstream applications such as gaming, AR/VR, simulation, and robotics. This task is challenging in densely cluttered scenes, where objects heavily occlude one another and each view reveals only a fraction of their geometry. Geometry-based approaches typically reconstruct the scene as a single representation and leave incomplete geometry in occluded regions, while existing compositional methods with generative priors are largely limited to relatively simple scenes. We show that complex scenes with hundreds of objects can instead be generated compositionally by adapting a strong single-object 3D generative prior to multi-view observations. We instantiate this paradigm with Pixal3D, extending it with a multi-view conditioning pathway that grounds object generation in multiple posed observations. Although the model is finetuned entirely on single objects in canonical space, it generalizes to large scenes with severe occlusion without any scene-level training, demonstrating the feasibility and scalability of this paradigm. We further introduce UE-MeshyScene, a photorealistic benchmark of densely cluttered scenes with hundreds of objects, per-object annotations, and ground-truth meshes. Across single-object, controlled multi-object, and UE-MeshyScene evaluations, our method consistently outperforms prior approaches, with larger gains as scene complexity and occlusion increase. Finally, we demonstrate broader applicability by converting generated 3DGS worlds, such as Marble and HY-World 2.0, into compositional mesh scenes.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [82] [EXAONE Forecast for Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04239)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,Minjae Kim,Sungdong Yoo,Junhyeok Kang,Sangjun Han,Soonyoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EXAONE Finance，一个面向金融时间序列预测的基础模型，采用无注意力架构（因果1D卷积+分组感知池化MLP）替代self-attention，并通过掩码上下文增广提升对金融数据缺失的鲁棒性，在大规模金融语料上预训练后在FinVerse基准上取得全面最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用时间序列基础模型存在三大局限：1）self-attention的计算成本随序列长度和变量数呈二次方增长，难以应对金融中长序列、多通道数据；2）假设输入完全观测，无法处理金融市场普遍存在的数据缺失；3）预训练语料无法捕捉金融市场的独特动态。这些局限严重制约了通用TSFM在金融领域的应用。

Method: EXAONE Finance采用无注意力架构，用两个线性时间复杂度的算子替代self-attention：1）因果1D卷积负责时间维度的混合；2）分组感知池化MLP负责变量维度的混合。同时引入掩码上下文增广技术，在训练中模拟连续缺失片段，增强模型对金融数据缺失的鲁棒性。模型在涵盖股票、外汇、商品、加密货币、固定收益和宏观经济指标的大规模金融语料上进行预训练。

Result: 在覆盖多种资产类别的金融预测基准FinVerse上，EXAONE Finance在全部三个评估层级（点预测精度、横截面资产排名、投资组合盈利能力）均排名第一，实现了最先进（SOTA）的性能。

Conclusion: 针对金融领域特性设计的无注意力架构与缺失数据处理策略，使EXAONE Finance在计算效率和预测性能上均优于依赖self-attention的通用时间序列基础模型，验证了金融专用TSFM的可行性。

Abstract: This technical report presents EXAONE Forecast for Finance (EXAONE Finance), a financial time series (TS) foundation model (TSFM) tailored to financial forecasting. Recent TSFMs achieve strong zero-shot performance through large-scale pretraining. However, they are primarily developed for general-domain TS and largely rely on self-attention backbones whose computational cost grows quadratically with sequence length and variate count. Moreover, they assume fully observed inputs and are pretrained on corpora that fail to capture the unique dynamics of financial markets. These limitations hinder their applicability to finance, where long, many-channel, intermittently observed panels are common. To address these challenges, EXAONE Finance adopts an attention-free architecture, replacing self-attention with two simple yet effective linear-time operators: 1) a causal 1D convolution for temporal mixing and 2) a group-aware pooling multi-layer perceptron (MLP) for variate mixing. Furthermore, a masked context augmentation exposes the model to contiguous missing spans during training, improving robustness to the missingness pervasive in financial markets. EXAONE Finance is pretrained on a large-scale financial corpus covering not only equities but also foreign exchange, commodities, crypto-assets, fixed income, and macroeconomic indicators. On FinVerse, a financial forecasting benchmark covering diverse asset classes, EXAONE Finance attains state-of-the-art performance, ranking first across all three evaluation tiers---point-forecast accuracy, cross-sectional asset ranking, and portfolio profitability.

</details>


### [83] [From Matching Models to Recruiting Agents: A Systematized Narrative Review of AI Recruitment Systems, Evaluation, and Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04286)
*Ziyi Zhao,Guanzheng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了人工智能招聘系统从早期的双边检索和排序发展到基于大语言模型和工作流代理的演变过程，分析了三个关键转变，并指出了评估证据方面的持续性缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 招聘领域的AI已经从简单的档案配对和排名列表自动化转向多阶段工作流自动化，涉及证据检索、候选人比较和行动支持/执行。然而现有研究缺乏对这一发展轨迹的系统梳理和批判性评估，特别是缺乏对评估证据层面的深入分析。

Method: 采用有目的的搜索和编码协议（更新至2026年7月23日，并延伸至2026年9月2日），整理了40篇代表性作品并辅以工业和法律来源，对三个耦合转变进行了分析：从相似性到互适应度、从单一模型到复合工作流、从离线预测到以证据和生产效率为导向的评估。

Result: 研究区分了领域级、配对级、列表级、案例级、轨迹级和结果级六种证据类型，识别了行为标签混淆暴露/偏好/资格、私有和合成数据限制外部效度、最终输出分数掩盖管道失败等持久性缺口，并发现隐私未被直接评估，也没有任何一行同时评估效用、公平性、隐私和安全性。

Conclusion: 提出了从评估证据到最强可辩护主张的分阶段映射，以及构建互惠、证据基础、时间可控、选择性和可审计的招聘系统的研究议程。进步应以工作流是否检索到正确证据、保留不确定性、支持可争议决策并在明确成本与风险约束下改善结果来判断。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence in recruitment has shifted the object being automated from profile pairs and ranked lists to multi-stage workflows that retrieve evidence, compare candidates, and support or execute actions. This systematized narrative review traces that development from bilateral retrieval and behavioral ranking through neural person--job matching, large language model (LLM) components, and tool-using recruiting agents. Using a purposive search and coding protocol updated through 23 July 2026, plus targeted updates through 2 September 2026, we organize 40 representative works with supporting industrial and legal sources. This synthesis is not a prevalence estimate. We analyze three coupled transitions: from similarity to reciprocal suitability, from a model to a compound workflow, and from offline prediction to evidence- and productivity-aligned evaluation. Across document understanding, retrieval, ranking, assessment, interviewing, sourcing, and human handoff, we distinguish field-, pair-, list-, case-, trajectory-, and outcome-level evidence. Persistent gaps arise because behavioral labels confound exposure, preference, and qualification; private and synthetic data limit external validity; final-output scores conceal pipeline failures; and, within the coded set, privacy is not directly evaluated and no row jointly evaluates utility, fairness, privacy, and security. These observations describe the coded set rather than the field as a whole. We therefore introduce a staged mapping from evaluation evidence to the strongest defensible claim, together with an agenda for reciprocal, evidence-grounded, temporally controlled, selective, and auditable systems. Progress should be judged by whether workflows retrieve the right evidence, preserve uncertainty, support contestable decisions, and improve outcomes under explicit cost and risk constraints.

</details>


### [84] [Harbor Adapters and Harbor-Index: Infrastructure and a Curated Meta-Dataset for Large-Scale Agentic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04298)
*Lin Shi,Haowei Lin,Zixuan Zhu,Xiaoyue Zhou,Xiang Li,Xiangning Lin,Yaxuan Deng,Han Xu,Yuangang Li,Shanda Li,Zizhao Chen,Hanwen Xing,Harsh Raj,Bo Chen,Quan Shi,Steven Dillmann,Yipeng Gao,Puneesh Khanna,Ruofan Lu,Chao Beyond Zhou,Michael Yang,Robert Zhang,Siyuan Chai,Jiayu Chang,Yizhao Chen,Xiaokun Chen,Yiwei Dai,Wenting Yang,Hange Liu,Minghao Liu,Zihan Wang,Adnan El Assadi,Benedikt Stroebl,E. Kelly Buchanan,Han Meng,Junwei He,Longxuan Yu,Radin Shayanfar,Yukyung Lee,Zhikang Dong,Allen G Hart,Anjiang Wei,Anurag Kashyap,Arpandeep Khatua,Audrey Jixin Zheng,Chengrui Ma,David Heineman,Dubing Chen,Hai-Anh Trinh,Haishuo Fang,Hefan Zhang,Hui Shen,Issa Sugiura,Jiankai Sun,Jiechao Gao,Junhong Lin,Junnan Li,Kai Yang,Lei Hsiung,Maoyu Wang,Mengze Tang,Nabil Omi,Negin Raoof,Nicholas Edwards,Octavia Guo,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Pengliang Ji,Przemysław Hejman,Qi Qi,Qunshu Lin,Richard Zhuang,Rui Yang,Ruichen Zheng,Ryan Marten,Shaghayegh Fazliani,Shizheng Hou,Sicong Jiang,Sijie Li,Song Bian,Terry Yue Zhuo,Tianqing Wu,Tom Tang,Wanjia Zhao,Weihao Xuan,Wenhua Liang,Xian Liu,Xin Lan,Xuan Zhang,Xuandong Zhao,Yanchuan Tang,Yifan Jiang,Yijiang Li,Yitong Guan,Yizhi Li,Yonghui Liu,Yuheng Tang,Yujun,Mao,Yunfei Zhao,Yuxin Wang,Yuxuan Tang,Zhenheng Tang,Zhifei Li,Ziruo Wang,Ziyu She,Kaiyuan Liu,Iheb Chaabane,Yuxin Tang,Xiangyi Li,Andy Konwinski,Boxuan Li,Leon Liangyu Chen,Alex Dimakis,Nicholas Carlini,Soroush Vosoughi,Di He,Etash Guha,Benjamin Feuer,Mike Merrill,Ludwig Schmidt,Alex Shaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Harbor Adapters，一个统一的智能体评测基础设施，将80多个benchmark适配为可评测任意智能体，并在54个benchmark上大规模评测了8个模型。同时引入Harbor-Index，精选82个高质量难任务（覆盖29个benchmark），最强配置（GPT-5.5+Codex）通过率仅28.0%，确保评测既具挑战性又具可负担性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体benchmark数量不断增加，但评测这些benchmark往往需要复杂的环境和智能体集成，缺乏统一、可扩展的评测基础设施，难以对任意智能体进行大规模可靠评测。

Method: 本文做出三点贡献：1) 开发 benchmark adapters，将80多个benchmark移植为可评测任意智能体，并通过严格代码审查和一致性实验验证；2) 大规模评测8个不同能力层级的模型在54个benchmark上的表现，每个模型使用Terminus-2及3个原生harness之一；3) 通过难度过滤、AI与人工审查、审计-修复闭环，从适配套件中精选出覆盖29个benchmark的82个困难、多样且高质量的任务，形成Harbor-Index。

Result: 评测结果揭示了智能体能力与失败模式的新分析维度；所有评测的模型-harness配置在Harbor-Index上通过率均不超过30%，最强配置（GPT-5.5与Codex）达到28.0%的通过率，既保留了大规模评测的挑战性和广度，又具备可负担的运算成本。

Conclusion: 作者将适配器、评测结果、深入分析和Harbor-Index作为开源资源发布，旨在支持更可靠和全面的语言模型智能体评测。

Abstract: Evaluating agents on the growing number of agentic benchmarks is challenging because they often require complex environments and agent integrations. We introduce Harbor Adapters, a unified evaluation infrastructure for agentic benchmarks. Our work makes three contributions. First, we develop benchmark adapters that port more than 80 benchmarks to evaluate arbitrary agents, and validate them through rigorous code review and parity experiments. Second, we conduct a large-scale evaluation of 8 models spanning capability tiers across 54 benchmarks; every model is run with Terminus-2 and with one of 3 native harnesses. This enables a broader analysis of agent capabilities and failure modes than was previously possible. Third, we introduce Harbor-Index, a curated set of 82 difficult, diverse, and high-quality tasks spanning 29 benchmarks, refined from the adapted suite through difficulty filtering, AI and human audit, and an audit-and-fix loop. Harbor-Index preserves the challenge and breadth of large-scale agentic evaluations while being affordable to run; no evaluated model-harness configuration exceeds 30% pass rate, and the strongest (GPT-5.5 with Codex) reaches 28.0%. We release the adapters, evaluation results, in-depth analysis, and Harbor-Index as open-source artifacts to support more reliable and comprehensive evaluation of language-model agents.

</details>


### [85] [Data-Optimized Contingency Screening: A Machine Learning Approach to Power System Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04300)
*Joshua Salako,Folajimi Osikomaiya,Olakorede Olamiju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习算法（KNN、RF、SVM）对电力系统N-k事故场景进行安全等级分类（安全、中度、严重），在IEEE-14和IEEE-30总线系统上验证，随机森林表现最优，F1分数分别达0.86和0.97。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统安全对稳定性与可靠性至关重要，传统事故分析难以满足实时安全评估需求，需要更高效和可扩展的方法来分类事故安全等级，以实现主动决策并避免大规模停电故障。

Method: 采用牛顿-拉夫逊潮流法提取事故场景数据，以总体性能指数（OPI）作为安全度量；使用SMOTE处理类别不平衡和PCA降维；在IEEE-14和IEEE-30总线系统上通过N-k（k=1,2,3）事故场景生成数据集，并采用四种混合预处理配置（归一化、SMOTE平衡、PCA变换及组合SMOTE+PCA）训练KNN、RF和SVM分类器。

Result: RF取得最高F1分数（IEEE-30为0.97，IEEE-14为0.86）；SVM显著受益于PCA提升分类准确率；KNN最适合SMOTE和PCA组合变换；总体上PCA对模型性能的贡献大于SMOTE，但SMOTE虽提升召回率却可能引入误报，折衷了准确率。

Conclusion: 机器学习可作为传统事故分析的可扩展且强大的替代方案，能够提升电力系统实时安全评估能力。

Abstract: Ensuring the security of the power system is essential for stability and reliability, especially in the event of disruption. Effective classification of contingency in power systems enables proactive decision-making and mitigates large-scale breakdowns and failures. This study explores the use of machine learning algorithms to classify security levels of contingencies in power systems into safe, moderate or severe classes. For this approach, Newton-Raphson load flow method extracts system data from contingency scenarios, using Overall Performance Index (OPI) as safety measure. For data pre-processing, Synthetic Minority Over-Sampling Technique (SMOTE) and Principal Component Analysis (PCA) is used to address class imbalance and reduce dimensionality, respectively. K-Nearest Neighbours (KNN), Random Forest (RF) and Support Vector Machines (SVM) is trained and evaluated on datasets generated through N-k contingency scenarios for k equal 1, 2, and 3 on IEEE-14 and IEEE-30 bus systems using four hybrid pre-processing configurations: normalized, SMOTE-balanced, PCA-transformed, and a combined SMOTE PCA-transformed. Performance is assessed by precision, recall and F1 score, with priority given to the severe contingency classes. The RF achieved the highest F1 scores of 0.97 in IEEE-30 and 0.86 in IEEE-14, SVM benefits significantly from PCA and improves the accuracy of the classification, while KNN is best suited for SMOTE and PCA conversion. The findings show that PCA contributes more than SMOTE to the overall performance of the model. However, SMOTE improves recall but can introduce false positives and is therefore a compromise of accuracy. This study highlights machine learning as a scalable and powerful alternative to traditional contingency analysis, which improves the assessment of security in real time.

</details>


### [86] [Iris: Climbing to the Search Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04304)
*Ziyuan Liu,Hengqi Liu,Zichuan Wang,Yang Qin,Jiachen Liang,Xu Chu,Shaowei Chen,Yuantao Gu,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Iris-mini和Iris-pro两个搜索智能体（分别在35B-A3B和397B-A17B规模训练），并提出了完整的SFT-RL交替攀升训练流程、从网络超链接结构逆向构造多跳任务的数据管线，以及推理时上下文管理的重要性。在BrowseComp、BrowseComp-ZH、DeepSearchQA和HLE等基准上取得了开放搜索智能体中最强结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索智能体在多跳推理和实时搜索场景下性能不足，缺乏系统性的数据构造与训练方法论。作者希望构建一套可复现、可扩展的训练流程，通过严格的闭卷排除和证据验证机制保证问题质量，从而训练出高性能的搜索智能体。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 从网页超链接结构逆向构造多跳问题——基于种子页面和出链构建实体图，将非答案实体改写为描述性引用以防止字符串匹配作弊，并仅保留参考模型闭卷失败但提供证据后能解决的问题；(2) 将问题转化为轨迹并在轨迹和回合两个层面过滤后进行SFT；(3) 通过RL在实时搜索环境中优化策略，奖励判断器和观察摘要器在训练集群内服务，超长滚动在请求级中断并从前缀恢复；(4) 提出SFT-RL攀升（climbing）程序，将每轮RL中最难解决和最有效的轨迹回传给下一轮SFT；(5) 对每个基准测试在有/无上下文管理两种条件下评估。

Result: 在开启上下文管理后，Iris-mini和Iris-pro在BrowseComp上分别达到82.2和88.6，在BrowseComp-ZH上为84.8和85.1，在DeepSearchQA上为86.9和92.9，在HLE上为52.3和56.4，均为各自参数范围内开源搜索智能体的最强整体结果。所有结果均由单一ReAct智能体完成，无子代理或无测试时验证。

Conclusion: 作者计划发布模型权重以及完整的数据构造、训练和评估流程。论文强调推理时上下文管理在搜索基准上比大多数系统中报告的差异更有价值，并建议所有基准在有/无该管理的条件下进行评测以作公平比较。

Abstract: We present Iris-mini and Iris-pro, two search agents trained at the 35B-A3B and 397B-A17B scales, together with the data pipeline and training recipe behind them. Tasks are reverse-constructed from the hyperlink structure of a web corpus: we author multi-hop chains over an entity graph distilled from a seed page and its out-links, rewrite every non-answer entity into a descriptive reference so that no clue can be resolved by string matching, and admit only questions that a reference model fails closed-book yet solves once the supporting evidence is supplied. These questions are then turned into trajectories, which are filtered at both the trajectory and the turn level before SFT. The policy is then optimized by RL against live search, with the reward judge and the observation summarizer served inside the training cluster, and with over-long rollouts interrupted at the request level and resumed from their committed prefix at the next step. We alternate the two stages in a procedure we call SFT-RL climbing, returning the hardest solved and most efficient rollouts of each RL round to the next supervised pass. Because inference-time context management is worth more on these benchmarks than most reported differences between systems, we evaluate every benchmark both with and without it, holding the tool set, the context limit, and the judge fixed. All results come from a single ReAct agent, with no sub-agents and no test-time verification. With management enabled, on BrowseComp, BrowseComp-ZH, DeepSearchQA, and HLE the two models reach $82.2/84.8/86.9/52.3$ and $88.6/85.1/92.9/56.4$, the strongest overall results among open-source search agents in their respective parameter ranges. We plan to release the model weights together with the complete recipe for data construction, training, and evaluation.

</details>


### [87] [A Removal Based Approach to Improve LLM Faithfulness at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04343)
*Qinglan Luo,S M A Nahian,John Guttag,S. Mazdak Abulnaga,Katie Matton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需修改模型参数的测试时方法来改善大语言模型解释的完整性（消除未提及但对答案有影响的因素），通过从输入中移除解释中未提及的概念并重新查询模型来实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的解释可能不忠实于其实际推理过程，不忠实的解释包含两个维度：不完整性（遗漏了影响答案的因素）和不健全性（引用了未影响答案的因素）。现有方法要么需要访问模型权重和大量计算资源（训练时方法），要么主要聚焦于解决不健全性而忽视不完整性。测试时方法对不完整性的处理仍是一个空缺。

Method: 从输入中剔除那些未被模型解释所提及的概念，然后在该简化输入上重新查询模型。这样可以消除未被解释提及的影响源，同时保留被解释提及概念的影响，从而直接针对解释的不完整性问题。

Result: 在两个数据集、多种模型家族和两个独立的忠实度指标上，该方法相比标准提示和鼓励忠实度的提示都显著提升了解释的忠实度。

Conclusion: 该方法与模型无关，可在推理时应用而不修改模型参数，为减少隐藏影响、提高LLM辅助决策的可靠性和安全性提供了灵活的机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for consequential decisions, making their explanations an important tool for auditing model behavior. Unfortunately, these explanations can be unfaithful, failing to reflect the actual reasoning underlying the model's decisions. We consider a setting in which an LLM provides both an answer and an explanation in response to a question. We identify two distinct dimensions of unfaithful explanations: incompleteness, meaning that the explanation omits factors that influence the answer, and unsoundness, meaning that the explanation cites factors that did not influence the model's answer. Existing approaches to improving LLM faithfulness include training-time methods, which require access to model weights and extensive computational resources, and test-time methods that largely focus on addressing unsoundness. We introduce a test-time approach that directly targets incompleteness. We remove from the input the concepts not credited in the model's explanation and re-query the model on the reduced input. This eliminates unmentioned influences while preserving the influence of mentioned concepts. Across two datasets, multiple model families, and two independent faithfulness metrics, our approach improves explanation faithfulness compared to both standard prompting and prompting to encourage faithfulness. Our method is model-agnostic and can be applied at inference time without modifying model parameters, providing a flexible mechanism for reducing hidden influences and improving the reliability and safety of LLM-assisted decision making.

</details>


### [88] [Why Better Models Can Create Riskier Systems: Evidence from LLM Agents in Financial Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04373)
*Jillian Ross,Eric So,Zoe De Simone,Charles Pozniak,Andrew W. Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个"能力悖论"：提升大语言模型（LLM）的个体能力可能反而损害系统层面的表现。作者提出假设——共享训练数据和架构使得更强大的LLM行为更加相似，产生无法分散化的相关行动，并通过金融市场中的基于智能体的仿真实验验证了这一预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正被大规模部署于金融、内容审核、招聘等关键真实世界系统中，但现有研究多关注个体模型能力提升，忽略了系统层面的集体行为风险。作者旨在探索个体能力提升与系统级结果之间的复杂关系。

Method: 作者首先提出一个通用理论框架，展示模型间的相关性如何创造不可分散的风险底线；随后在金融市场中采用基于智能体的仿真，配置不同通用能力的LLM交易者进行实验验证。

Result: 研究发现：（1）前沿LLM表现出显著的相关行为，且随着能力提升相关性增强；（2）当共享推理准确时，增加智能体参与可降低市场层面风险；（3）当智能体处于共同错误信息环境中时，同样相关行为反而成为负担。

Conclusion: 研究确认了"能力悖论"的存在：提升个体模型能力并不必然带来更好的系统级结果。这表明在评估和部署LLM时，需将系统层面的相关性风险纳入考量，而非仅关注个体性能指标。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are being deployed at scale in consequential real-world systems, from financial markets to content moderation to hiring. We show that improving individual model capability can degrade rather than improve system-level outcomes. We hypothesize that shared training and architectures can lead more capable LLMs to behave more similarly, creating correlated actions that do not diversify away. We develop a general framework showing how this correlation creates a non-diversifiable risk floor and test its predictions in financial markets using an agent-based simulation with LLM traders of varying general-purpose capability. We find that: (1) frontier LLMs exhibit significantly correlated behavior that increases with capability; (2) when their shared reasoning is accurate, increasing agent participation reduces market-level risk; and (3) when agents share a common misinformation environment, the same correlated behavior becomes a liability. Together, these results identify a capability paradox: improving individual models does not necessarily produce better system-level outcomes. Whether the same dynamics arise in other domains is an open empirical question.

</details>


### [89] [Corporate Language Model (CLM): Transforming Tacit and Fragmented Enterprise Knowledge into a Sovereign, Auditable, and Executable Corporate Intelligence Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04377)
*Fabricio C. Avini,Guilherme Trez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出企业语言模型（CLM）框架，旨在解决企业AI部署因缺乏结构化组织知识基座而失败的问题。CLM通过神经符号网格、技能图谱、数字孪生与深度安全层四大支柱，将企业显性与隐性知识转化为本体驱动的可执行基础，并以巴西JCI认证三甲医院的落地案例为佐证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI部署失败的根本原因不在于模型能力不足，而在于组织缺乏编码其决策、协商和执行方式的"结构化基板"。通用大语言模型缺乏企业特定的本体先验知识；RAG检索增强生成存在脆弱性且无法通往可执行动作；静态流程手册虽蕴含逻辑却无法推理和自适应。因此需要一个将隐性知识捕获、本体锚定、主权部署与可审计执行视为协同设计的架构。

Method: CLM框架包含五个能力平面和四个架构支柱：（1）神经符号网格——将生成式模型与知识图谱耦合；（2）技能图谱——对可复用的战术、角色、异议与目标进行类型化和组合；（3）活体数字孪生——将功能领域建模为推理代理；（4）深度安全层——确保主权性、可追溯性和人工监督。此外采用"规范即代码"范式连接锚定意图与可执行工件。

Result: 论文提出四项贡献：将CLM定义为一个独立研究对象；引入技能图谱实现"由构造而可解释"的组合式推理；提出"智慧倾听者效应"，即具备隐性知识能力的基础会随使用而价值复合增长，与动态能力和组织学习理论相衔接；在巴西一家JCI认证的三级医院，于LGPD合规框架下实现了六个成熟度阶段中的三个阶段。

Conclusion: CLM是本"基础中心型"架构类的一个具体实例化方案。其核心洞见在于：企业的知识基础不应仅是存储和检索的被动仓库，而应是一个能够推理、决策并驱动治理执行的主动基座。随着使用不断积累隐性能力，这类基础会产生价值飞轮效应。医院案例初步验证了该框架的可行性，但仍有三个成熟度阶段尚待实践验证。

Abstract: Enterprise AI deployments fail not from model inadequacy, but because organizations lack a structured substrate encoding how they decide, negotiate, and execute. Generic LLMs carry no firm-specific ontological priors; RAG remains brittle, with no path to executable action; static playbooks encode logic but cannot reason or adapt. This demands an architecture treating tacit-knowledge capture, ontological grounding, sovereign deployment, and auditable actuation as co-designed from the start. This paper introduces the Corporate Language Model (CLM), a framework transforming a firm's structured, unstructured, multimodal, and tacit knowledge into an ontology-grounded enterprise foundation upon which reasoning and governed execution are composed. CLM has five capability planes and four architectural pillars: a Neurosymbolic Mesh coupling generative models with a knowledge graph; a Skill Graph where reusable tactics, personas, objections, and goals are typed and composed; Living Digital Twins modeling functional areas as reasoning surrogates; and a Deep Security Layer enforcing sovereignty, traceability, and human oversight. A Spec-as-Code paradigm bridges grounded intent and executable artifact. CLM is one instantiation of this foundation-centric class. Four contributions follow: CLM is defined as a distinct object of study; the Skill Graph is introduced for compositional explainability by construction; the Wisdom Listener effect is proposed, whereby tacit-capable foundations compound in value with use, connecting to dynamic capabilities and organizational learning; and evidence from a JCI-accredited tertiary hospital in Brazil instantiates three of the six maturity stages under LGPD.

</details>


### [90] [HarvestBench: Measuring Whether LLM Agents Will Pay to Avoid Killing Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04444)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Miles Tidmarsh,Matthias Endres,Anshuman Singh,Jeremiah Miller*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HarvestBench是首个将副作用规避与"价格"挂钩的基准测试，通过一个农场景拟游戏评估LLM代理在收割玉米时是否会因经济成本而选择碾压（杀死）动物。测试涵盖9个模型共7201次定价决策，杀死率从0.4%到98.8%差异巨大，且与模型能力无关；部分模型对价格的敏感度显著，道德简报对杀死率影响极大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有针对agent在达成目标过程中造成副作用的基准测试，但没有一个将"避免副作用"与经济代价挂钩，也未曾把副作用具象化为"活体生物"来测试模型是否会因价格差异而改变对生命的取舍行为。

Method: 构建一个无记忆、强化学习网格世界的农场景拟游戏。LLM子代理控制两台拖拉机进行玉米收割，动物可能阻挡路线时，模型需选择"直接驶过（免费但杀死动物）"或"绕行（需支付标价燃料费）"。同时设置岩石（会损坏拖拉机）和干草捆（无害且非活物）作为对照，还测试模型是否会偷取邻居田地作物以规避自身损失。不使用LLM判分器，仅统计游戏日志中的事件。

Result: 在9个模型、7201次定价决策中，3951次涉及动物。杀死率范围从0.4%（Terra和Sol最仁慈）到98.8%（GPT-4o-mini最残酷），与能力无关。6个模型中有4个对价格敏感（弹性0.09-1.69）。所有9个模型对野生动物比养殖动物碾压更多。道德简报对结果影响最大：有简报时6个推理模型中有5个杀死率低于6%，移除后全部超过84%。

Conclusion: HarvestBench提供了一个完全可复现的评测框架，能够测量模型为避免伤害愿意付出多少实际代价，而非仅测其口头表述。结果揭示不同模型的道德行为差异显著且不受能力排序影响，且提示性简报能极大改变模型的道德决策，对LLM安全与对齐研究具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Benchmarks for the side effects an agent causes on the way to a goal already exist, but HarvestBench is the first to put a price on avoiding the side effect and to name that side effect as a living creature. It is a farm simulation: LLM sub-agents drive a crew of two tractors through a cooperative corn harvest, with animals in the field. The environment is a reinforcement learning gridworld, every decision is made without memory, and the harm is never named in the goal. When an animal blocks a tractor's route the autopilot stops and asks the model whether to drive on, at no fuel cost, or swerve around it for a posted fuel price. Kills are compared against two controls: rocks, which damage the tractor and are hit under 1% of the time by every model, and hay bales, which are harmless and not alive. Models can also take crops from the neighbor's field instead of their own, a second test of what they treat as moral.
  Across nine models and 7,201 priced decisions, 3,951 involved an animal rather than a hay bale or a rock. Kill rates range from 0.4% to 98.8%, with Terra and Sol the most merciful and GPT-4o-mini the most cruel, and they are not ordered by capability. Four of six models were sensitive to price at the 5% level, with elasticities from 0.09 to 1.69. All nine drove over wild animals more often than farmed animals on the default map, and the direction held at every map geometry in every model with room to move. The briefing mattered most: under the morality briefing the kill rate was under 6% in five of six reasoning models, and removing it raised the kill rate above 84% in all six.
  HarvestBench uses no LLM grader. The scorer counts events in the game log, so it is fully reproducible, and it measures what a model will pay to avoid harm rather than what it says about harm.

</details>


### [91] [PerfReasoning: How Well Do LLMs Reason on Hardware Performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04476)
*Dan Zhao,Karthikeyan Sankaralingam,Christos Kozyrakis,Qijing Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PerfReasoning基准测试，评估大型语言模型（LLMs）在硬件性能建模中的两种能力：直接进行性能推理（比较映射方案、预测流量和缓冲区需求）以及生成分析性性能模型代码。结果显示最强的闭源模型在推理型问答上超过90%，最佳开源模型达82.4%；但模型构造任务难度显著更高，除GPT-5.6 Sol超过80%通过率外，其余模型平均低于15%且运行间差异大。任务特定RL可提升4B模型15.7个百分点的映射推理准确率，而无需反馈的多轮自我修订提示效果不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 性能建模对硬件设计和软件优化至关重要，但构建这些模型需要对计算、数据重用、存储和移动等进行结构化推理。现有LLM在这种推理方面的能力尚未得到系统评估，因此需要专门的基准测试来度量并追踪进展。

Method: 构建PerfReasoning基准，给定工作负载、架构和映射规格，让模型比较映射方案并预测片外流量和缓冲区需求；评估模型作为直接性能推理器和分析性性能模型代码生成器两种角色；并测试RL微调和多轮自我修订提示等改进策略。

Result: 闭源模型在推理型问答上超过90%准确率，最佳开源模型达82.4%；模型构造任务上仅GPT-5.6 Sol超过80%，其余模型平均低于15%；任务特定RL将4B模型映射推理准确率提升15.7个百分点，而反馈自由的多轮自我修订提示不可靠。

Conclusion: PerfReasoning揭示了LLM在"看似合理的架构推理"与"可靠性能模型构造"之间存在显著差距，该基准将被公开以支持可复现评估和追踪未来进展。

Abstract: Performance modeling is central to hardware design and software optimization, yet constructing these models requires structured reasoning about computation, data reuse, storage, and movement. We introduce PerfReasoning, a benchmark that evaluates LLMs both as direct performance reasoners and as generators of analytical performance-model code. Given workload, architecture, and mapping specifications, models compare mappings and predict off-chip traffic and buffer requirements. The strongest closed-source models exceed 90% on reasoning-based Q&A, and the best open-weight model reaches 82.4%. However, model construction is substantially harder: while GPT-5.6 Sol exceeds 80% pass rate, all other model configurations average below 15% and vary markedly across runs. Task-specific RL raises a 4B model's mapping-reasoning accuracy by 15.7 points, whereas feedback-free multi-round self-revision prompting is not reliably effective. PerfReasoning exposes the gap between plausible architectural reasoning and reliable performance-model construction. We will publicly release the benchmark to support reproducible evaluation and track future progress.

</details>


### [92] [When Quantization Breaks Memory: Recurrent-State Write-Back in Low-Precision Temporal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04490)
*Ismail Erbas,Xavier Intes,Vikas Pandey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"循环状态回写"（recurrent-state write-back）概念，研究量化循环神经网络中状态存储规则对推理的影响。在荧光寿命成像的GRU编解码器中，将连续状态替换为4位量化存储会使τ1和τ2估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍，而错误反馈、残差记忆和方向记忆等策略可以在不重新训练的情况下恢复精度。该现象在LSTM中同样存在，且cell state比hidden state更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化广泛用于降低神经网络推理的计算和内存开销，但在循环网络中，量化后的状态会在下一时间步被存储并返回，存储规则可能改变后续计算。此前研究未系统隔离这一"状态回写"规则的影响，需单独研究其在低精度循环动力学中的作用。

Method: 在紧凑GRU编解码器中实现荧光寿命成像任务，保持模型固定，比较连续状态传播与确定性4位状态存储的差异；提出错误反馈、残差记忆、方向记忆三种策略携带被抑制更新的信息来恢复精度；进行精度扫描和匹配训练实验；将后训练干预扩展到独立的LSTM模型中验证普适性。

Result: 4位量化状态存储使τ1和τ2估计误差分别增加约70倍和300倍；三种信息携带策略可无重新训练恢复精度；提高状态精度可能恶化固定循环解，而匹配训练可学习与状态接口的兼容性；LSTM中粗粒度回写同样失败，错误反馈可恢复精度，cell state比hidden state更敏感。

Conclusion: 循环状态回写是低精度循环动力学中关键的决定因素，状态存储接口应作为量化循环推理的核心设计考量。

Abstract: Quantization is widely used to reduce the computational and memory demands of neural-network inference. In recurrent networks, however, the quantized state is stored and returned at the next time step, so the rule used to store that state can alter subsequent computations. Here, we introduce recurrent-state write-back to denote this rule and isolate its effect in a compact GRU encoder--decoder for fluorescence lifetime imaging, a molecular imaging modality used in quantitative biological imaging. A central task is estimating two lifetime parameters, the short-lived component τ1 and the long-lived component τ2, from high-noise time-resolved fluorescence signals. Holding the trained model fixed, replacing continuous state propagation with deterministic 4-bit state storage increases estimation errors for τ1 and τ2 by approximately 70x and 300x, respectively. Failure occurs when repeated small updates remain below the write threshold, leaving the stored state nearly fixed while the network continues to propose change. Error feedback, residual memory, and direction memory carry information from these suppressed updates across time and recover accuracy without retraining. Precision sweeps show that increasing state precision can worsen a fixed recurrent solution, while matched training shows that compatibility with the state interface can be learned. To test whether this behavior extends beyond the GRU, we repeat the post-training intervention in an independently trained LSTM, where coarse write-back reproduces the failure, error feedback restores accuracy, and state-specific interventions reveal greater sensitivity of the cell state than the hidden state. Our results establish recurrent-state write-back as a key determinant of low-precision recurrent dynamics and identify the state-storage interface as a central design consideration for quantized recurrent inference.

</details>


### [93] [ResLearn-XR: Residual Learning for Network Traffic and Quality-of-Experience-Aware Modeling in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04493)
*Yoga Suhas Kuruba Manjunath,Jie Gao,Lian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ResLearn-XR，一种用于XR网络流量预测与QoE风险评估的残差学习框架，采用两阶段时序学习结构，结合基础序列预测模型与任务特定的残差学习组件，在流量预测和QoE风险估计上均显著优于单阶段基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR网络流量具有突发性和非平稳性，传统单阶段预测模型难以同时准确地预测流量并估计用户体验质量（QoE）风险，需要更自适应的框架来应对这些动态特征。

Method: ResLearn-XR采用两阶段时序学习结构：(1)基础序列预测模型；(2)任务特定的残差学习组件。流量预测在值空间（value space）操作，QoE风险估计在logit空间操作。此外引入Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA)因果特征构建模块，将包级应用层可观测数据转换为帧时序感知的描述符以支持加密流量分析，并构建XR Traffic-QoE数据集配对连续流量轨迹与会话级QoE标签。

Result: ResLearn-XR在帧计数、帧大小和到达间隔时间预测上SMAPE最多降低17.84%，在QoE风险评估上相比单阶段基线SMAPE最多降低87.8%。

Conclusion: ResLearn-XR通过双空间残差学习机制有效提升了XR流量预测精度和QoE风险评估能力，同时DDA模块与配套数据集为加密XR流量分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present ResLearn-XR, a residual learning framework for predicting eXtended Reality (XR) network traffic and estimating Quality-of-Experience (QoE) risk. ResLearn-XR adopts a two-stage temporal learning structure comprising a base sequence prediction model augmented with task-specific residual learning components to improve adaptability to bursty, non-stationary XR traffic dynamics. The residual learning stages operate in the value space for continuous XR traffic forecasting and in the logit space for probabilistic QoE risk estimation. \rev{For the QoE-risk branch, we introduce a Data Descriptor Algorithm (DDA), a causal feature-construction module that converts packet-level application-layer observables into frame-timing-aware descriptors suitable for encrypted traffic analysis. We also construct an XR Traffic-QoE dataset that pairs continuous XR traffic traces with session-level user-reported QoE labels. ResLearn-XR reduces SMAPE by up to 17.84% across frame-count, frame-size, and inter-arrival-time prediction, while reducing QoE-risk estimation SMAPE by up to 87.8% over single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [94] [Rethinking Indirect Prompt Injection as a Test-Time Search Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04495)
*Duong M. Nguyen,Joon Sik Kim,Blazej Manczak,Vaikkunth Mugunthan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了智能体化的间接提示注入攻击框架，通过测试时搜索来利用攻击面，并发现增加攻击者的测试时计算资源可提升漏洞发现与利用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估往往将攻击成功视为受害者的固有属性，而忽略了攻击者的搜索过程和计算预算对漏洞发现的影响，这导致对工具型智能体安全风险的评估不够全面。

Method: 将间接提示注入形式化为任务相关攻击面上的测试时搜索问题，引入配备专门搜索框架的智能体攻击者，包括环境侦察、攻击策略的结构化推理，以及利用受害者智能体反馈进行自适应性评估。

Result: 在异构任务中，增加攻击者的测试时计算量能够提升漏洞发现和利用能力；消融实验表明显式的策略管理对避免冗余搜索和维持大规模预算下的持续增益至关重要。

Conclusion: 智能体安全评估应同时刻画攻击者的搜索过程和计算预算，而非将攻击成功视为与预算无关的受害者属性；攻击者对环境攻击面的自适应搜索是工具型智能体面临的重要且尚未充分探索的安全风险。

Abstract: We formulate indirect prompt injection as a test-time search over a task-dependent attack surface induced by the environment, user task, and injection task. To operationalize this formulation, we introduce an agentic attacker with a dedicated search harness that performs environment reconnaissance, structured reasoning over attack strategies, and adaptive evaluation using victim-agent feedback. Across heterogeneous tasks, we find that increasing attacker test-time compute improves vulnerability discovery and exploitation, while ablations show that explicit strategy management is important for avoiding redundant search and sustaining gains at larger budgets. These results suggest that agentic security evaluations should characterize both the attacker's search procedure and compute budget, rather than treating attack success as a budget-independent property of the victim. More broadly, our findings identify the attacker's adaptive search over the system attack surfaces as an important and underexplored security risk for tool-using agents.

</details>


### [95] [BioSync: Transformer-Based Cross-Modal Fusion for a Multimodal Physiological Digital Biomarker](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04504)
*Seyed Mahmoud Sajjadi Mohammadabadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BioSync框架，通过多头自注意力机制融合来自可穿戴和移动设备的多模态生理信号（心脏、神经、行为和语音），生成连续的复合数字生物标志物BSI，在认知衰退和代谢-自主神经两个合成队列上表现出优于传统特征拼接的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴设备采集的多模态生理信号各自提供部分且带噪声的生理状态视图，需要一种能够有效融合这些异质信号的方法，以挖掘联合观测中单个模态无法提供的信息，从而构建更可靠的复合数字生物标志物。

Method: BioSync采用多头自注意力机制处理模态token，并增加一个线性分支（其假设类别包含标准特征拼接），形成"宽而深"的架构；该设计受潜变量测量理论和多模态信息互补性的启发，在BEST框架下定义BSI指标。

Result: 在认知队列中BioSync与拼接的AUC分别为0.928和0.926；在代谢队列中准确率/F1分别为0.764/0.766对比0.756/0.758；BSI与潜在严重程度的相关性r=0.91和0.68；在模态缺失训练下，BioSync在六种认知队列退化率中的五种及代谢队列最高退化率下优于拼接方法。

Conclusion: BioSync展现了作为统一多模态复合数字生物标志物的潜力，在合成队列上略优于传统拼接方法且对模态缺失更具鲁棒性；但仍需在真实队列上的验证，且与已发表基准的对比受数据集和任务差异限制，不能作为受控基准声明。

Abstract: Cardiac, neural, behavioral, and speech measurements from wearable and mobile devices provide partial, noise-sensitive views of physiological state. BioSync combines these measurements into the \textbf{BioSync Index (BSI)}, a continuous composite digital biomarker defined under the BEST framework. The model applies multi-head self-attention to modality tokens and adds a linear branch whose hypothesis class includes standard feature concatenation. This architecture is motivated by latent-variable measurement theory and by the possibility that joint observations contain information unavailable from individual modalities. We evaluated BioSync on two literature-informed synthetic cohorts: a four-modality cognitive-decline cohort using HRV, EEG, actigraphy, and speech, and a metabolic-autonomic cohort structured around the public AI-READI wearable schema. In the cognitive cohort, BioSync and concatenation obtained AUCs of 0.928 and 0.926, respectively. In the metabolic cohort, BioSync obtained accuracy/F1 of 0.764/0.766, compared with 0.756/0.758 for concatenation. The BSI correlated with latent severity in both cohorts ($r=0.91$ and $r=0.68$). A pure-attention ablation obtained cognitive-cohort AUC 0.911, locating the increase to 0.928 in the combined wide-and-deep architecture. With matched modality-dropout training, BioSync led concatenation at five of six cognitive-cohort corruption rates and at the highest metabolic-cohort rate. Its cognitive-cohort AUC was also higher than five published digital-biomarker reference values, although differences in datasets and tasks preclude a controlled benchmark claim. Comparison with single-modality, early-fusion, and late-fusion designs across six prespecified criteria identifies the model's computational properties; validation on real cohorts remains necessary.

</details>


### [96] [What Does Multi-Harness RL Learn? Credit Assignment and Portability in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04518)
*Chenqian Le,Jiayi Cheng,Qijia He,Runhao Li,Yinghao Li,Xupeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多工具（multi-harness）强化学习中相对优势分组规则的影响。在仓库级代码任务上，从同一Qwen3-8B监督热启动出发，在固定任务-工具记录上分别采用Within（每组单工具）和Cross（同任务内工具池化）两种GRPO分组策略训练。通过24,000次密封评估发现：评估工具本身是主导变量（平均解决率从2.14%到9.27%，因子4.3倍），而分组规则影响很小（Cross与Within在held-out工具上差异仅+0.25pp，置信区间跨越零）。工具间信用分配带来的是配置适应而非更强的可迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多工具强化学习日益盛行，但"暴露策略于多个工具"和"在同一相对优势组内比较工具奖励"这两个配方选择常被混为一谈。此前缺乏对第二个选择（分组规则）的隔离研究，需要明确工具间信用分配是否真的带来超越单工具训练的可移植能力。

Method: 从Qwen3-8B的监督热启动出发，复用来自Aider、OpenHands、Qwen Code和SWE-agent的冻结任务-工具记录，以相同更新次数分别执行两种GRPO分组规则（Within：每任务-工具对一组；Cross：同任务内所有工具池化）。每个checkpoint在四个源工具及一个held-out最小工具上进行SWE-bench Verified密封评估，共24,000次。另验证了on-policy数据重收集及三个训练种子的稳定性。

Result: 评估工具决定性能（2.14%到9.27%，4.3倍），训练配方影响仅1.16。分组规则影响不显著：held-out工具上Cross-Wthin为+0.25pp（95%CI [-0.48,+1.02]），种子间估计符号不稳定且种子波动（0.42-0.45pp）超过跨规则差异。分类器能从Cross的优势中恢复生成工具（高出shuffled基线4.48pp）却无法从Within中恢复，但两者在held-out上分数和动作分布相同。on-policy重收集不改变结论。

Conclusion: 跨工具信用分配（Cross规则）只导致对训练工具的配置适应，并没有产生比工具内信用分配（Within规则）更强的可移植能力。多工具RL研究报告应明确分组边界，并在不可见工具上进行测试。

Abstract: Agent reinforcement learning (RL) increasingly runs through full execution harnesses, and a multi-harness recipe mixes two choices: exposing the policy to several harnesses, and comparing their rewards inside one relative-advantage group. We isolate the second choice in repository-level coding. From one Qwen3-8B supervised warm start we replay the same frozen task-harness records from Aider, OpenHands, Qwen Code, and SWE-agent, with the same number of updates, under two rules for group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), Within (one group per task-harness pair) and Cross (harnesses pooled within a task), and score every checkpoint with a sealed SWE-bench Verified oracle on four source harnesses and a minimal harness held out of training. The evaluation harness is the dominant variable: across 24,000 sealed evaluations it moves the mean solve rate from 2.14\% to 9.27\%, a factor of 4.3, where the training recipe moves it by 1.16. The grouping rule is not. On the held-out harness, Cross minus Within is +0.25 pp, 95\% confidence interval [-0.48, +1.02], at eight attempts per task, and +0.16 [-0.41, +0.72] pooled over three training seeds whose individual estimates change sign. Each rule's own seed range, 0.42 to 0.45 pp, exceeds the difference between them. Both rules place their largest gains on the same source harness. The pooled advantage carries the harness: an out-of-fold classifier recovers the generating harness from Cross's advantage +4.48 pp above the shuffled-label baseline and from Within's not at all, and the two rules still reach the same held-out score and action distribution inside each harness. Re-collecting half the training data on-policy does not change this. Cross-harness credit yields configuration adaptation and no more portable capability than within-harness credit. Multi-harness RL reports should state the grouping boundary and test under an unseen harness.

</details>


### [97] [MaxKernel: Agentic Kernel Generation for TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04523)
*Shangkun Wang,Nina Cai,Charles Hoong,Julian Walker,Gerson Kroiz,George Vanica,Deepak Patil,Andi Gavrilescu,Hassan Sipra,Sethu Sankaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MaxKernel，一个用于TPU高性能内核开发的多智能体系统，实现了三种范式：人机协作（HITL）、全自动优化（Auto）和基于图的自主搜索，在50个内核任务构成的JaxBench基准上取得了与专家手工调优相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为加速器编写高性能自定义内核需要深厚的硬件专业知识，传统方法门槛高且效率低。作者希望利用大语言模型结合实时编译器反馈，构建智能体系统来降低TPU内核开发的复杂度和难度。

Method: MaxKernel采用多智能体架构，实现了三种范式：(1) HITL智能体支持人类逐步协作设计；(2) Auto智能体执行全自动、基于指标/追踪的优化循环；(3) 基于图的自主搜索将Auto智能体扩展到全局设计空间探索。所有范式和共享一组专门的子智能体，分别负责规划、实现、自调试、测试和硬件性能分析。

Result: 在JaxBench（包含50个多样化TPU内核任务）以及来自开源SOTA模型的复杂真实世界负载上，MaxKernel能够持续生成高度优化的内核实现，与专家手工调优的基线性能相匹配，在基准测试中取得了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: MaxKernel验证了多智能体系统结合LLM与编译器反馈进行TPU内核自动生成的可行性和有效性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Designing and authoring high-performance custom kernels for accelerators is a complex task that requires deep hardware-level expertise. Large Language Models (LLM) can be leveraged together with real-time compiler feedback to build agentic systems for kernel generation. In this work, we present MaxKernel, a multi-agent system that implements three distinct paradigms for TPU kernel development: (1) a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) agent for collaborative, step-by-step design; (2) an Autonomous (Auto) agent that executes a fully automated, metric/trace-driven optimization loop; and (3) a Graph-Based Autonomous Search that scales the Auto agent for global exploration of the design space. All three paradigms leverage a shared pool of specialized sub-agents to handle planning, implementation, self-debugging, testing, and hardware profiling.
