Both mediators are similarly easy to use, although the human expert is perceived as more reliable. These findings demonstrate the potential of voice agents to reduce expert involvement while identifying perceived reliability and preference generalization as remaining challenges.

</details>


### [198] [Dressing in Motion: A Human Motion-Aware Diffusion Policy for Robot-Assisted Dressing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04759)
*Haoxiang Sun,Fangyuan Wang,Songhao Huang,Justina Y. W. Liu,Jihong Zhu,Peng Zhou,David Navarro-Alarcon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于扩散策略的视觉运动策略，用于机器人辅助穿衣任务。该方法从静态专家演示中学习穿衣技能，并通过基于PDE扩散的物体中心表征来捕捉手臂轴向分布，从而泛化到动态人体运动场景，实现实时轨迹自适应调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助穿衣是帮助行动不便老年人日常生活的有前景方案，但在人体运动下穿衣仍具挑战性，因为复杂的服装-人体接触和遮挡使得生成与手臂运动对齐的动作变得困难。

Method: 提出一种针对服装-人体交互几何的扩散策略，从部分观察的点云（含多变手臂姿势）中学习。引入基于PDE扩散的物体中心表征来捕捉手臂轴向分布，通过采样运动相关区域并在连续观测间进行配准，估计手臂运动并反应式地调整执行轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实人体研究中评估，涉及九名参与者、三种服装类型和六种手臂运动模式。结果显示该方法在穿衣进展、运动自由度和用户舒适度方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够从静态演示中学习穿衣技能并有效泛化到动态人体运动场景，在多个评估维度上优于现有基线方法，验证了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Robotic dressing assistance is a promising solution for supporting older adults with physical impairments in daily living. However, dressing under human motion remains challenging, as complex garment--human contact and occlusions make it difficult to generate actions aligned with arm movements. In this letter, we propose a visuomotor policy that learns dressing skills from static expert demonstrations and generalizes to dynamic user-motion scenarios. A diffusion policy tailored to garment--human interaction geometry learns from partially observed point clouds with varied arm postures. We then introduce an object-centric representation based on PDE diffusion to capture the axial distribution of the arm. By sampling motion-relevant regions and registering them across consecutive observations, the proposed method approximates arm motion and reactively adapts the executed trajectory. We evaluate our method in simulation and a real-world human study involving nine participants, three garment types, and six arm-motion patterns. Results show that our method outperforms baselines in dressing progress, freedom of movement, and user comfort. The project website is https://anonymous.4open.science/w/dressing-in-motion.

</details>


### [199] [Continuous Cognitive Coverage for Autonomous Robots via Event-Dependent Cognitive Treatment and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04770)
*Hong Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种连续认知覆盖框架，使每个被认知接纳的事件都能根据其状态、上下文和历史获得差异化的事件依赖认知处理（如描述、记忆、风险预测、规划、诊断、类比等），支持多认知过程的挂起、恢复与交错执行，并通过持续学习自动化和优化事件专属处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务驱动、反应式或固定推理的方法通常只处理选择性事件或应用预定义的推理过程，难以在持续面对新事件的环境中提供具有差异化处理的连续认知覆盖，许多事件在无明确任务时得不到及时的认知处理。

Method: 提出连续认知覆盖框架：每个被认知接纳的事件依据其状态、上下文和历史被赋予事件依赖的认知处理；熟悉事件由学习机制自动处理，陌生或不确定事件触发显式思考或回退推理；支持多认知过程的挂起、恢复和交错以确保持续处理；通过验证经验的持续学习来自动化、细化和修订事件专属处理。

Result: 实验达到96.76%的结构化处理准确率、93.66%的自动处理率、92.64%的爆发-延迟工作负载下的认知覆盖率，以及79.53%的持续学习联合准确率，新事件复用场景下自动处理率达100%。

Conclusion: 该框架能够有效为每个认知事件提供差异化且持续的处理覆盖，在保证高准确率的同时大幅提升自动化处理比例，并通过持续学习不断优化事件专属认知处理策略。

Abstract: Autonomous robots continuously encounter objects, changes, and situations, and every event admitted into cognition should receive an appropriate cognitive treatment rather than remain untreated until an explicit task requires attention. However, existing task-driven, reactive, or fixed-reasoning approaches generally process only selected events or apply predefined reasoning procedures, making it difficult to provide continuous cognitive coverage with differentiated treatment. This paper proposes a continuous cognitive coverage framework in which every cognitively admitted event is assigned an event-dependent cognitive treatment according to its state, context, and history. Different events may therefore invoke description, memory, risk prediction, planning, diagnosis, analogy, or other learned treatments. Familiar events can be processed automatically by learned mechanisms, whereas unfamiliar or uncertain events invoke explicit deliberation or fallback reasoning. Multiple cognitive processes can be suspended, resumed, and interleaved so that cognitive processing continues as new events arrive or existing events await evidence. Validated experiences are continuously learned to automate, refine, and revise event-specific treatments. Experiments achieve 96.76% structured treatment accuracy with 93.66% automatic processing, 92.64% cognitive coverage under bursty-delayed workloads, and 79.53% continual-learning joint accuracy, with novel-event reuse reaching 100% automatic processing.

</details>


### [200] [HaptiNet: Networked Haptic Robots Enable Physical Co-presence in Geographically-Unconstrained Rehabilitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04799)
*Chenyang Sun,Mingjie Dong,Haodong Deng,Yudong Liu,Yi-Feng Chen,Jun Lin,Changlong Huang,Jie Guo,Yantong Liu,Yang Liu,Yuzhou Lin,Jianjun Long,Zheng Xing,Sining Zhao,Xuemin Zhang,Zhiyong Wang,Zhenhong Li,Dongrui Wu,Honghai Liu,Jian S. Dai,Mingming Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HaptiNet 是一种网络化触觉机器人系统，通过力介导交互实现远程用户的物理共存，用于上肢神经康复训练。该系统在284名健康参与者和111名神经损伤患者中验证，相比独自或视觉协作训练，触觉协作将任务表现提升24%和22%，并在跨约4000公里的三个城际网络中保持稳定交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统康复训练中的协作需要物理同在，而远程康复目前以视听交互为主，患者在手感和物理层面被隔离，缺乏触觉交互带来的参与度、任务表现和社交运动协调方面的益处。

Method: 提出HaptiNet系统，包含低惯性长行程高力反馈末端执行器，用于上肢训练的触觉渲染；在此基础上构建分布式触觉网络，采用基于模仿学习的延迟补偿器，使用户能在远距离上物理感知与协调。

Result: 在284名健康参与者和111名神经损伤患者的三类场景（实验室、跨城市部署、临床应用）中验证。触觉协作相比独自训练任务表现提升24%，相比视觉协作提升22%，同时增强参与度和人际运动同步。跨三个城际链路约4000公里，患者基线到训练的分数提升为独自条件的3.87倍，患者施加努力提高106%。

Conclusion: HaptiNet证明了远程触觉协作康复的可行性和有效性，为地理分散的患者提供了物理共存式康复体验，弥补了远程康复中触觉隔离的空白。

Abstract: Cooperative rehabilitation enhances engagement, task performance, and social-motor interaction, yet it demands physical co-presence: users must transmit forces, coordinate movements, and infer intent through haptic contact. Telerehabilitation promises to expand access for patients constrained by distance, mobility, or clinical disparities, yet current techniques remain predominantly audiovisual while leaving users haptically and physically isolated. Here, we introduce HaptiNet, a networked haptic robotic system enabling physical co-presence for geographically distributed users via force-mediated interaction. Each robotic terminal features a low-inertia, long-stroke design with high force-feedback capacity, tailored for haptic rendering in upper-limb training. Building on these terminals, HaptiNet creates a distributed haptic network with an imitation-learning-based delay compensator, enabling users to physically perceive and coordinate with one another over distance. We validated HaptiNet in 284 healthy participants and 111 patients with neurological impairments across progressively realistic settings, including laboratory tests, cross-city deployments, and clinical applications. HaptiNet preserved task-level force rendering consistency across single-user and multi-user scenarios. Compared with solo and visual cooperative training, haptic cooperation improved task performance by 24% and 22%, respectively, while also boosting engagement and interpersonal motor synchrony. Across three intercity links totaling approximately 4,000 km, HaptiNet maintained stable haptic interaction among patients with neurological impairments, producing a 3.87-fold greater baseline-to-training score improvement and a 106% higher patient-applied effort over the solo condition.

</details>


### [201] [CoLMIN: LLM-based Multi-Decision Path Negotiation for Cooperative Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04807)
*Zhe Huang,Zhaoxin Fan,Shuo Wang,Wenjun Wu,Xuan Zhao,Min Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoLMIN是一个基于LLM的多决策路径协商框架，用于协同自动驾驶，通过多意图协商和反思推理实现稳定且高质量的决策共识，在CARLA仿真中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的协商方法在复杂交通场景中由于决策本质上是多解的，容易过早收敛到次优解，阻碍共识形成并限制协同自动驾驶系统的实际部署。

Method: CoLMIN包含三个核心模块：(i) LMin——基于LLM的多意图协商模块，采用Negotiator-Evaluator范式，生成多个候选驾驶意图进行联合评估；(ii) ESRM——基于评估的浅层反思模块，分析协商结果并提供反馈以加速共识形成；(iii) LDRM——基于LLM的深层反思模块，对协商历史进行长期反思以缓解认知固着，防止系统收敛到次优解。

Result: 在CARLA仿真环境中进行的实验表明，CoLMIN在具有挑战性的交互式驾驶场景中显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过多决策路径协商与双重反思机制的结合，CoLMIN能够有效避免过早收敛至次优解，实现稳定、高质量的驾驶决策共识，为协同自动驾驶的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-vehicle cooperative autonomous driving enhances the safety and reliability of autonomous driving systems through information sharing among connected vehicles, demonstrating significant potential for improving traffic safety. LLM-based approaches leverage strong reasoning capabilities of LLMs to enable effective inter-vehicle negotiation and improve cooperative driving performance. However, driving decisions in complex traffic scenarios are inherently multi-solution in nature. As a result, existing negotiation-based methods often converge prematurely to suboptimal solutions, hindering consensus formation and limiting the practical deployment of cooperative autonomous driving systems. To address this challenge, we propose CoLMIN, the LLM-based multi-decision path negotiation framework for cooperative autonomous driving, achieving stable decision consensus through multi-decision path negotiation and reflective reasoning. To achieve stable and high-quality consensus in cooperative autonomous driving, CoLMIN consists of three key components: (i) an LLM-based Multi-Intent Negotiation module (LMin), which adopts a Negotiator-Evaluator paradigm and generates multiple candidate driving intentions for joint evaluation; (ii) an Evaluation-based Shallow Reflection Module (ESRM), which analyzes negotiation outcomes and provides feedback to guide subsequent negotiations, thereby accelerating consensus formation; and (iii) an LLM-based Deep Reflection Module (LDRM), which performs long-term reflection over negotiation histories to mitigate cognitive fixation and prevent the system from converging to suboptimal solutions. Experimental results in the CARLA simulation environment demonstrate that CoLMIN significantly outperforms existing methods in challenging interactive driving scenarios.

</details>


### [202] [Strict Modes Everywhere - Bringing Order Into Dynamics of Mechanical Systems by a Potential Compatible With the Geodesic Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04817)
*Arne Sachtler,Alin Albu-Schäffer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计了能使配置空间中密集填充严格非线性模态的势能函数，使每个配置点属于一个模态且每个模态通过平衡点。该势能可通过非线性弹性元件实现，从而高效执行周期性轨迹，大部分所需力矩来自弹性元件而非执行器，并通过控制器和实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格的非线性正常模态在保守机械系统中提供非常规则的振荡族，但通常只是配置空间中的孤立曲线，限制了其实际应用范围。

Method: 设计一种能够使配置空间密集填充严格非线性模的势能函数，使每个配置属于一个模态且每个模态通过共同平衡点；设计控制器稳定到目标模态，以及执行摆动和补偿能量耗散的控制器。

Result: 在二自由度机械臂上进行了实验验证，实验表明该方法在示例系统中表现良好。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的势能（可由非线性弹性元件实现），可以使系统以极高效的方式执行多种周期轨迹，大部分所需力矩来自弹性元件而非执行器。

Abstract: Strict nonlinear normal modes provide very regular families of oscillations within conservative mechanical systems. However, a strict normal mode will generally be an isolated curve within the configuration space of the system. In this letter, we design a potential that will densely fill the configuration space with strict normal modes such that each configuration belongs to one mode and each mode passes through a common point, the equilibrium. As the potential can be realized by (nonlinear) elastic elements it can be used to execute a variety of periodic trajectories very efficiently. Most of the required torques will come from the elastic elements in the system and not from the actuators. We also design a controller stabilizing the system to a desired target mode and a controller performing swing-up and compensating dissipated energy. Finally, we showcase the approach for a two DoF manipulator. The experiments show that the approach performed well for the example system.

</details>


### [203] [Coupled Control and Wireless World Models for Resilient Remote Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04851)
*H. P. Madushanka,Sumudu Samarakoon,Mehdi Bennis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于耦合并控制与无线JEPA世界模型的远程机器人鲁棒控制框架，通过联合预测机器人状态与无线信道条件实现通信感知的预测性调度，在减少上行传输的同时保持可靠控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程机器人系统在无线网络上运行面临通信资源受限、信道条件变化和环境干扰等多重挑战。持续传输高维感知数据（如图像）会显著增加通信开销和能耗，同时在连接不稳定时降低系统鲁棒性，亟需一种能够兼顾通信效率和可靠控制的框架。

Method: 提出基于耦合并控制与无线JEPA世界模型的远程机器人控制框架。该框架从视觉观测、频谱图和持久性图像（PIs）等原始及结构化射频表示中联合学习机器人动力学与无线信道演化规律；通过学习到的潜空间表示对机器人状态和无线条件进行联合预测，实现预测性通信调度；并设计自适应弹性机制，通过检测潜空间预测差异来动态调整感知嵌入以应对环境变化，无需重新训练完整控制策略。评估在同步的Gazebo-ROS-Sionna机器人-无线仿真环境中进行。

Result: 在多种无线传播和感知扰动条件下，相比传统PID、无模型深度Q网络（DQN）以及基于Vision Transformers（ViTs）的预测方法，该框架在通信效率、鲁棒性和弹性方面均取得显著提升，同时维持了相当的导航性能。

Conclusion: 耦合控制与无线JEPA世界模型能够有效实现远程机器人控制的通信感知调度，为资源受限无线环境下的远程机器人系统提供了兼顾可靠性与效率的解决方案。

Abstract: Remote robotic systems operating over wireless networks must maintain reliable control despite limited communication resources, changing channel conditions, and environmental disturbances.However, continuously transmitting high-dimensional sensory observations, such as camera images, increases communication overhead and energy consumption while reducing robustness under unreliable connectivity.To address these challenges, this paper proposes a resilient communication-aware remote robotic control framework based on coupled control and wireless Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) world models that jointly capture robot dynamics and wireless channel evolution from visual observations and a combination of raw and structured radio frequency (RF) representations based on spectrograms and Persistence Images(PIs).The learned latent representations enable predictive communication scheduling by jointly forecasting future robot states and wireless conditions, thereby reducing unnecessary uplink transmissions while maintaining reliable control performance.Furthermore, an adaptive resilience mechanism detects latent prediction discrepancies and efficiently adapts perception embeddings to accommodate wireless and visual environmental changes without retraining the complete control policy.The proposed framework is evaluated in a synchronized Gazebo-Robot Operating System (ROS)-Sionna robot-wireless simulation environment under diverse wireless propagation and perception perturbations.Experimental results demonstrate significant improvements in communication efficiency, robustness, and resilience while maintaining navigation performance compared with conventional Proportional Integral Derivative (PID), model-free Deep Q-Network (DQN), and predictive approaches based on Vision Transformers(ViTs).

</details>


### [204] [Reasoning Without Inference Cost: Latent Semantic Scaffolding for Robot VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04893)
*Andrew Ting Yan Li,Zhuo Li,Zhelin Yang,Zhipeng Dong,Quentin Rouxel,Fei Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在语义脚手架（LSS），一种在人类演示预训练期间应用的辅助损失，用于将VLA模型的动作特征与物理推理理由的文本嵌入对齐。关键发现是逐阶段密集对齐（Dense LSS）优于整体池化对齐（Pooled LSS），且推理时无需额外成本，因为投影头在推理阶段被丢弃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型通过模仿学习训练，只捕获"做什么"而不理解"为什么"。现有的因果关系推理方法需要在推理时生成推理token或滚动预测未来状态，这会导致计算成本随任务时域增长而累积。作者希望将这些益处转移到训练阶段获得，推理时无需额外开销。

Method: 提出Latent Semantic Scaffolding（LSS），在人类演示预训练期间通过一个小型投影头，将VLA模型的动作token表示与物理推理理由的文本嵌入对齐。该投影头在推理时被丢弃。论文区分了两种对齐粒度：Dense LSS（每个动作token与其所在操作阶段的理由对齐）和Pooled LSS（整个episode使用单一池化嵌入对齐）。

Result: Dense LSS在分布内任务上取得最佳成功率，且在训练对齐中未见过的任务上迁移效果最好；而Pooled对齐过度特化于训练任务。表征探针实验表明Dense LSS在骨干网络中诱导出约两倍的逐阶段可分性，证明阶段局部对齐是关键机制。

Conclusion: 通过对齐粒度的精细控制（逐阶段而非整体池化），可以在训练阶段将物理推理能力编码进VLA模型表征中，实现零额外推理成本的同时获得更好的分布内外表现与迁移能力，为VLA模型的高效因果推理提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are trained by imitation and capture what action to take but not why; adding causal reasoning improves manipulation, but current methods pay for it at inference time - generating reasoning tokens or rolling out predicted future states at every step, a cost that compounds over long horizons. We ask whether this benefit can instead be captured during training and discarded before deployment. We introduce Latent Semantic Scaffolding (LSS), an auxiliary loss applied during human-demonstration pretraining that aligns a VLA's action-token representations to text embeddings of physical-reasoning rationales through a small projection head. The head is dropped at inference, leaving the unmodified base policy with zero added cost. Our central finding concerns alignment granularity: aligning each action token to the rationale of its own manipulation phase (Dense LSS) rather than to a single pooled episode-level embedding (Pooled LSS) yields representations that transfer markedly better to held-out tasks. Dense LSS attains both the best in-distribution success and the best transfer to tasks unseen during alignment, whereas pooled alignment over-specializes to the training task. A representational probe shows Dense LSS induces roughly twice the per-phase separability in the backbone, supporting that phase-local alignment is the operative mechanism.

</details>


### [205] [ToPos: Automated Optimal Positioning on Topographic Manifolds using Constrained Geodesic Voronoi Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05084)
*Rajesh Raveendran,Akseli Vanhamaa,Jaakko Suutala,Antti Tikanmäki,Juha Röning*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ToPos框架，一个面向地形感知最优采样的自动化系统。它将地形视为嵌入3D欧几里得空间的离散2维流形，用非欧几里得测地距离替代平面距离，通过约束测地Voronoi分解和Riemannian NAG优化引擎在安全可行区域内分布空间参考点，相比传统2D方法在表面面积均衡分布上提升约74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统2D欧几里得方法在复杂地形环境中忽视了地形起伏和物理障碍，导致显著的平面畸变、空间聚类以及目标点落入不可达或阴影区域，影响数据完整性和作业安全。因此需要一种考虑地表真实几何结构的采样方法。

Method: 将地形建模为嵌入3D空间的离散2维流形，使用测地距离替代平面距离；将点位分布构建为约束测地Voronoi分解的优化问题；通过黎曼Nesterov加速梯度(NAG)引擎求解；将目标位置限制在可行"安全区"内，排除不可穿越坡度、植被和环境遮挡等区域；框架以GIS可用的微服务架构实现。

Result: 在非凸正弦流形上的评估表明，ToPos通过测地度量缓解了平面畸变问题，在表面面积均衡分布(Voronoi单元面积系数变异CV)上实现了约74%的改进。

Conclusion: ToPos为自主测绘、环境采样、最后一公里物流和基础设施部署等应用提供了更可靠、更安全的空间参考点分布方案，其GIS-ready微服务架构便于实际集成与部署。

Abstract: Reliable autonomous mapping, environmental sampling, last-mile logistics, and infrastructure deployment depend on the optimal surface area-balanced distribution of Spatial Reference Sites (SRS). Conventional 2D Euclidean methods often fail in high-relief environments by neglecting topographic variations and physical obstructions. This leads to significant planimetric distortion, spatial clustering, and the placement of targets in inaccessible or shadowed regions, compromising both data integrity and operational safety. This paper introduces ToPos, an automated framework for TOPography-aware Optimal Sampling on topographic manifolds. We treat the terrain as a discrete 2-dimensional manifold embedded in 3D Euclidean space and replace standard flat-map distances with non-Euclidean geodesic distances that follow the actual surface geometry. The point distribution is formulated as an optimization problem using a Constrained Geodesic Voronoi Decomposition, solved via a Riemannian Nesterov Accelerated Gradient (NAG) engine. Our approach restricts target locations to a feasible "safe zone," accounting for non-traversable slopes, vegetation, environmental occlusions, etc. Through evaluations on non-convex sinusoidal manifolds, we show that ToPos mitigates planimetric distortion by utilizing geodesic metrics. This approach results in a $\sim$74% improvement in optimal surface area-balanced distribution, as measured by the coefficient of variation (CV) of the Voronoi cell areas. The framework is architected as a Geographic Information System (GIS)-ready micro-service to bolster the mentioned applications.
  Index Terms: Topographic Manifolds, Geodesic Voronoi Decomposition, Infrastructure Deployment, 3D Mapping, Spatial Sampling, and Non-Euclidean Optimization.

</details>


### [206] [A Schema Bounded Language Model for Refining Robot Policies Without Destabilizing Local Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05133)
*Chongwen Dong,Mithun Paul Saint-Germain,Pinjari Asif,Carlo R. daCunha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究异构机器人群体在去中心化系统中的导航问题，提出一种分层决策框架：LLM仅在回合级（round-level）进行策略生成与改进，UCB负责模式选择，Double DQN在tick级执行动作控制。三种不同LLM后端的机器人在NetLogo-Python仿真中协作导航，实验表明完整配置在固定仿真中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人群体导航系统通常依赖中央LLM或单一决策层级，难以兼顾策略推理的灵活性与实时控制的响应速度。本文旨在探索在去中心化系统中，如何让策略推理与局部控制在不同的更新频率（回合级与tick级）上协同工作，以提升异构机器人群体的导航效率。

Method: 在NetLogo-Python环境中部署三个共享运动学但采用不同LLM后端的机器人。每个机器人独立集成三层架构：(1) LLM策略代理负责回合级策略生成与改进；(2) UCB置信上界算法执行细化模式选择；(3) 策略条件化的Double DQN负责tick级动作选择，融合导航变量、活动策略参数及LLM动作先验。机器人通过共享回合摘要（包含策略、结果和学习反馈）实现跨LLM通信，无中央LLM生成群体动作。共评估四种配置，每种运行30个回合。

Result: 在固定仿真环境中，完整配置在90个机器人-回合记录中全部成功到达目标，中位完成时间为42 ticks（P90为73.2 ticks），其中位时间比其他配置低25.0%–39.1%，体现了分层决策与跨LLM协作的有效性。

Conclusion: 该分层架构——回合级LLM策略推理与tick级强化学习控制相结合——在去中心化异构机器人导航中展现了可行性与性能优势。作者谨慎地将结果限定为"配置级描述性证据"，未做因果推断，表明后续需要更严谨的对照实验以验证方法的普遍适用性。

Abstract: This paper addresses navigation by composite heterogeneous robots in a decentralized system when policy reasoning and local control operate at different update levels. In a NetLogo--Python implementation, three robots share motion dynamics but use different LLM backends. Each robot independently combines a large language model (LLM) policy agent, an Upper Confidence Bound (UCB) bandit, and a Double Deep Q-Network (Double DQN) controller; no central LLM generates team actions. LLM inference is confined to round-level policy generation and refinement rather than tick-level action selection. The robots perform cross-LLM communication through a shared round summary containing policies, outcomes, and learning feedback. UCB performs refinement-mode selection, and the policy-conditioned Double DQN performs tick-level action selection from navigation variables, active policy parameters, and the LLM action prior. Each of the four configurations was evaluated over 30 rounds. In the fixed simulation, the complete configuration reached the goal in all 90 correlated robot--round records and achieved the lowest median completion time (42 ticks) and P90 (73.2 ticks); its median was 25.0--39.1\% lower than those of the other configurations. These observations provide descriptive, configuration-level evidence from the evaluated configurations.

</details>


### [207] [LIBERO-RECOVER: Beyond Task Success Towards Failure Recovery in Robotic Manipulation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05178)
*Lin Liu,Zhicheng Bao,Lu Zhang,Ziying Song,Wu Yang,Shuai Tao,Wulong Liu,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LIBERO-Recover基准，一个大规模机器人操作失败恢复评估基准。该基准基于LIBERO构建，收集了1000+个失败恢复场景，涵盖四级恢复层次，将评估范式从"机器人能否成功"转向"机器人能否在失败后恢复"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA/WAM模型在LIBERO等基准上已接近100%成功率，但这类基准主要从预定义初始状态评估任务完成，忽略了真实世界中不可避免的失败（如抓取失败、碰撞、物体意外移动）。这导致基准性能与现实可靠性之间存在严重差距，而机器人的失败识别与恢复能力几乎没有被测量。

Method: 作者构建了LIBERO-Recover基准：基于LIBERO，从SOTA具身模型中收集真实执行失败，构建1000+个场景。场景分为四级恢复层次：(1)动作重试、(2)动作自适应、(3)物体状态恢复、(4)环境恢复。同时评估四项核心能力：空间理解、物体结构推理、交互理解和拓扑推理。

Result: 构建了首个大规模具身失败恢复基准LIBERO-Recover，包含1000+个跨四个恢复层级的失败场景，并定义了评估失败恢复所需的四项核心能力维度。

Conclusion: LIBERO-Recover将评估关注点从"能否成功"转移到"能否在失败后恢复"，填补了基准性能与现实可靠性之间的评估空白，推动更鲁棒和泛化的具身智能体发展。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) or World Action (WAM) models have recently demonstrated remarkable performance in robotic manipulation. On LIBERO, SOTA method have achieved nearly 100\% success rates, seemingly suggesting that the models are ready for deployment in real world. However, near perfect performance on existing benchmarks can be misleading: success under ideal conditions does not imply real world robustness. Existing benchmarks primarily evaluate task completion from predefined initial states, while real world interactions inevitably involve failures such as failed grasps, collisions, and unintended object movements. A robot must therefore not only execute tasks successfully, but also recognize and recover from failures to continue the task. Yet this capability remains largely unmeasured, revealing a critical gap between benchmark performance and real world reliability. To address this gap, we introduce LIBERO-Recover Benchmark, a large scale benchmark for failure recovery in robotic manipulation. Built upon LIBERO, we collect real execution failures from SOTA embodied models and construct 1,000+ scenarios across four recovery levels: (1) Action Retry, (2) Action Adaptation, (3) Object State Recovery, and (4) Environmental Recovery. We evaluate four core capabilities: spatial understanding, object structure reasoning, interaction understanding, and topological reasoning. As the first large-scale benchmark for embodied failure recovery, LIBERO-Recover shifts evaluation from \emph{Can the robot succeed?''} to \emph{Can the robot recover after failure?''}, promoting robust and generalizable embodied agents. The project will be avaible in \textcolor{blue}{https://liulin815.github.io/LIBERO-Recovery/}.

</details>


### [208] [Morphology and actuation as inductive biases in robotic hand manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05206)
*Zalán Tari,Eszter Birtalan,Péter Polcz,Miklós Koller*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一框架，分别分析灵巧手的运动学与驱动阶段及其组合，通过任务雅可比矩阵、驱动矩阵及其乘积的条件数来评估。将该框架应用于Shadow灵巧手和ACBH手两套代表性设计，从关节轴几何、驱动器-自由度比、耦合架构和权力分配四个形态学维度分析，并用PPO、DDPG+HER和TQC+HER强化学习实验验证指标预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人手在解剖保真度和机械复杂性上差异巨大，这些结构选择影响关节运动协调和系统控制难度，但缺乏一个统一的框架来系统比较不同设计哲学之间的控制效果。

Method: 将运动学和驱动阶段分别分析再组合，通过任务雅可比矩阵、驱动矩阵及其乘积的条件数评估。对手的关节轴几何、驱动器-自由度比、耦合架构和权力分配四个形态学方面，从两张手的规范数字表示中提取参数进行比较。

Result: 解剖保真度并无统一优势：倾斜轴改善拇指条件却使长手指条件更差；分支腱网络改善所有长手指的有效控制映射，但显著恶化拇指条件，且拇指驱动力集中在对抗运动上。指标预测通过PPO、DDPG+HER和TQC+HER在三个任务上的强化学习实验得到验证。

Conclusion: 结构选择对灵巧手控制的影响是高度局部化和多方面的，解剖学设计并非在所有维度上都优于正交轴设计，所提出的统一框架可作为灵巧手结构设计的有效分析工具，其指标预测能与强化学习实际性能相匹配。

Abstract: Robotic hands vary widely in anatomical fidelity and mechanical complexity, and these structural choices influence the coordination of joint motions and the difficulty of controlling the system. A unified framework is presented in which the kinematic and actuation stages are analysed separately and in composition, through the conditioning of the task Jacobian, the actuation matrix, and their product. It is applied to two hands representing opposing design philosophies, the Shadow Dexterous Hand and the Anatomically Correct, Biomechatronic Hand, along four morphological aspects: joint axis geometry, actuator-to-DOF ratio, coupling architecture, and authority distribution. All parameters are derived from the hands' canonical digital representations. Anatomical fidelity carries no uniform advantage: oblique axes improve thumb conditioning but leave the long fingers worse conditioned than the orthogonal-axis design, while the branching tendon network improves the effective control mapping at every long finger and worsens it significantly at the thumb, where actuator authority is concentrated on thumb opposition. Predictions derived from these metrics are evaluated against reinforcement learning experiments using PPO, DDPG+HER, and TQC+HER, across three different tasks.

</details>


### [209] [One Word, Different Action: A Real-Robot Benchmark for Language-Conditioned Embodied Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05260)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang,Shunbo Lei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"One Word, Different Action"（一个字，不同动作）基准，用于评估具身智能系统在指令变化下是否保持决策不变性或正确更新决策。实验发现现代模型在单约束指令变化上已趋近饱和，但在多约束组合推理中仍显著退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言指令变化能直接改变机器人行为，而可靠的具身系统应在任务未变时保持动作一致性、任务改变时正确更新动作。现有研究缺乏同时对决策不变性和决策敏感性进行联合评估的真实机器人基准。

Method: 构建基于物理决策状态和可执行动作的真实机器人基准，采用任务保持型和任务改变型指令对，联合评估"决策不变性"与"决策敏感性"，并在多约束推理和真实RGB定位条件下进一步评测。

Result: 现代模型在单约束指令变化任务上接近饱和，但多个模型在需要将多个任务约束整合为一个可执行决策时出现明显性能退化。

Conclusion: 当前具身决策面临的核心挑战已从识别孤立的指令变化，转向可靠地将多个任务约束组合成正确的机器人动作决策。

Abstract: Natural-language instruction changes can directly alter robot behavior. A reliable embodied system should preserve its action when the task is unchanged and update it correctly when the task itself changes. We introduce One Word, Different Action, a real-robot benchmark built on physical decision states and executable actions, using task-preserving and task-changing instruction pairs to jointly evaluate Decision Invariance and Decision Sensitivity, with further evaluation under multi-constraint reasoning and real-RGB grounding. Experiments show that modern models are near saturation on single-constraint instruction changes, yet several models degrade noticeably when multiple task constraints must be integrated into one executable decision. These results suggest that the more salient remaining challenge is no longer recognizing an isolated instruction change, but reliably composing multiple task requirements into a correct robot action decision.

</details>


### [210] [TacPAC: Tactile Prediction and Real-Time Action Correction in World-Action Models for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05266)
*Zipei Ma,Xiaofei Wei,Junzhe Jiang,Shunlin Lu,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacPAC通过将触觉预测转化为实时动作校正来提升接触丰富的操作任务表现，在五个真实机器人任务中平均成功率从22%提升至64%，且校正成本比重新生成动作块低20.7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界-动作模型依赖视觉中心预测，无法捕获决定接触丰富操作成败的局部接触线索。直接将触觉观测作为额外视图进行预测效果有限（仅恢复三分之一的可达增益），因为预测发生在执行之前而触觉反馈在执行期间到达，存在时间错配。

Method: TacPAC将触觉预测转化为实时动作校正：基础模型规划动作块后，系统缓存该规划所依据的预测接触信息及其规划表示；触觉专家将每个新观测到的触觉图像与该缓存进行比对，校正尚未执行的动作。反馈被置于规划预期的上下文中解释而非孤立分析，每次校正只需对缓存进行单次遍历。

Result: 在五个真实机器人任务（精密插入、易碎物体处理、物体重定向、长时程操作）中，TacPAC在所有任务上均取得领先，将视觉基线的平均成功率从22%提升至64%，且单次校正比重新生成动作块便宜20.7倍。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将触觉反馈与执行前的规划预期进行对比而非盲目预测触觉未来，是弥合触觉预测与实时操作之间时序差距的有效途径，为接触丰富的灵巧操作提供了高效且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: World-action models guide action generation with predicted future observations, but vision-centric predictions miss the local contact cues that decide contact-rich manipulation. However, naively predicting future tactile observations as additional views recovers only a third of the achievable gain in our experiments. This gap reflects a timing mismatch: predictions precede execution, while tactile feedback arrives during it. We introduce TacPAC, which turns tactile prediction into real-time action correction. Once the base model has planned an action chunk, TacPAC caches the predicted contact that plan was conditioned on together with the plan's own representation, and a tactile expert reads each newly observed tactile image against that cache to correct the actions not yet executed. Feedback is thus interpreted against what the plan anticipated rather than in isolation, and one correction is a single pass over that cache, $20.7\times$ cheaper than regenerating the chunk. On five real-robot tasks spanning precision insertion, fragile-object handling, object reorientation, and long-horizon manipulation, TacPAC leads every task and raises the average from 22% for its vision-only base model to 64%. Code is available at https://github.com/LogosRoboticsGroup/TacPAC.

</details>


### [211] [Temporal Tactile Encoding and Compliance for Intent-Aware Robot-to-Human Bimanual Handover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05282)
*Pasquale Marra,Stefano Berti,Gabriele Mario Caddeo,Lorenzo Natale*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将VLA模型与柔顺控制器相结合，利用RGB视觉、时序触觉反馈和本体感觉的融合来提升人机交接物体的可靠性与舒适性。实验表明，柔顺控制与时序触觉编码两个组件具有互补优势，显著优于无触觉或无柔顺控制的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人向人类交接物体时，仅靠视觉观察难以区分明确的接收意图与偶然接触、弱抓取、错误方向力或瞬时交互，导致释放时机不可靠且不舒适。现有方法缺乏对多模态信息的有效融合，尤其是触觉历史信息在捕捉持续接收意图方面的作用未被充分利用。

Method: 将人机交接建模为固有的多模态问题，使用VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型与柔顺控制器耦合。VLA模型通过人类演示进行微调，输入包含RGB观测、时序编码的触觉反馈以及本体感觉信息。柔顺控制器用于减少物体传递过程中的交互力。

Result: 在人体受试者研究中，所提出的完整系统（柔顺控制+时序触觉编码）相较于仅无触觉反馈和无柔顺控制的两个基线，在客观指标和问卷评估上均显著更优，两个组件显示出互补性的收益。

Conclusion: 柔顺控制促进物理交互，而时序触觉编码能捕获持续的接收意图，两者结合是可靠且舒适的人机交接的关键。验证了多模态融合（视觉+触觉时序+本体感觉）在处理交接意图模糊性问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Reliable robot-to-human handover requires the robot to infer when the person is ready to receive the object, and release it safely, comfortably, and at the right time. This is challenging because visual observations alone may not disambiguate clear taking intent from accidental contact, weak grasping, wrong-direction forces, or transient interactions. In this work we treat human-robot handover as an intrinsically multimodal problem. Our approach couples a VLA model with a compliance controller that reduces interaction forces during object transfer. We finetune the VLA model with human demonstrations using RGB observation, temporally encoded tactile feedback and proprioception. We evaluate the complete system in a human-subject study against two baselines: one without tactile feedback and one using tactile feedback without compliance control. We hypothesize that combining compliance and temporal tactile encoding yields the most reliable and comfortable handovers, as compliance facilitates physical interaction while tactile history captures sustained taking intent. Performance is measured through objective metrics and an ad-hoc questionnaire. The results show that the two components provide complementary benefits and substantially outperform the baselines. Code and data will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [212] [Human-Human & Human-Robot Interaction Transformer (H2INT) for Robot Navigation in Dense and Uncertain Crowds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05300)
*Ao Shen,Kaixi Chen,Shiwei Liu,Fang Deng,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出H2INT框架，一种在稠密人群中安全导航的强化学习方法，通过Transformer分层编码人际与人机交互，并允许人群对机器人的响应性可变，从而提升导航安全性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的行人运动预测方法通常假设行人运动独立于机器人，或假设行人对机器人具有均匀的互惠响应，忽略了交互不确定性的重要来源，导致密集人群中的导航不够安全。

Method: 提出Human-Human & Human-Robot Interaction Transformer (H2INT)强化学习框架：(1)保留机器人条件化行人运动变化，允许响应性因人而异；(2)采用两阶段门控Transformer分层编码人际与人机关系；(3)通过循环策略捕捉时序演化；(4)设计课程学习逐步降低行人响应性以增加交互难度。

Result: 仿真实验表明该方法在多种响应条件和人群密度下优于代表性基线，并可在无需重新训练的情况下迁移到结构不同的拥挤流场布局；消融实验验证了分层关系编码和门控更新的有效性；真实机器人部署验证了策略能在物理环境中以稀疏观测运行。

Conclusion: H2INT通过显式建模行人之间及人机之间的交互差异，有效提升了密集人群中的导航安全性与鲁棒性，并展示了良好的跨场景迁移能力和真实世界部署潜力。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in dense crowds requires reasoning about pedestrian motion and how it may change in response to a robot. However, many learning-based approaches generate pedestrian motion independently of the robot or assume uniform reciprocity, omitting an important source of interaction uncertainty. This paper presents a Human-Human & Human-Robot Interaction Transformer (H2INT), a reinforcement learning framework that retains robot-conditioned changes in pedestrian motion during policy learning while allowing responsiveness to vary across pedestrians. Responsiveness affects the crowd dynamics when the robot is visible but is not supplied as a policy input; the policy must instead infer its consequences from robot-centered relative positions. A two-stage gated Transformer progressively encodes human-human and human-robot relations, while a recurrent policy captures their temporal evolution. A curriculum gradually reduces pedestrian responsiveness to increase interaction difficulty. Simulation experiments demonstrate improved navigation safety and robustness over representative baselines across response conditions and crowd densities, and show transfer without retraining to structurally distinct crowd-flow layouts. Ablations support the hierarchical relational encoding and gated updates. Real-robot deployment further verifies that the learned policy can operate with sparse observations in a physical environment.

</details>


### [213] [RoboSPA: Can VLA Models Go Beyond Simple Scenes and Short-Horizon Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05324)
*Zhenxuan Fan,Bo Zhang,Yutong Lin,Yuqian Yuan,Juekai Lin,Liang Liang,Zhuoyi Huang,Wenqiao Zhang,Juncheng Li,Siliang Tang,Jun Xiao,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RoboSPA，一个大规模机器人操作数据集与基准，专门用于诊断VLA模型在细粒度空间推理和长时程程序规划方面的能力，涵盖10个任务类别、56个基础任务、280个难度变体和527K条轨迹，并引入超越二值成功率的诊断指标。实验表明当前VLA模型在复杂空间关系、精确低级执行和记忆密集型规划上仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA数据集和基准主要评估预定义设置下的任务完成情况，缺乏对模型在空间复杂度与程序复杂度递增条件下推理能力的深入诊断，无法揭示模型推理能力的短板。

Method: 构建RoboSPA数据集与基准，聚焦细粒度空间推理和长时程程序规划两个核心维度，跨10个任务类别、56个基础任务，每个任务包含5个难度级别（共280个变体），采集多种具身形态和场景下的527K条轨迹，并提供诊断性评估指标以超越简单的二值成功率。

Result: 对代表性VLA模型的实验表明，现有系统在复杂空间关系理解、精确低级动作执行以及需要记忆的长时程规划方面仍存在明显不足，RoboSPA成功揭示出当前模型的推理瓶颈。

Conclusion: RoboSPA作为一个具有挑战性的诊断基准，为推动更强大、更可靠、更可泛化的具身智能体开发提供了重要评估工具和基准平台。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown promising progress in language-conditioned robotic manipulation. However, existing datasets and benchmarks mainly evaluate task completion under predefined settings, offering limited insight into model reasoning under increasing spatial and procedural complexity. We introduce \textbf{RoboSPA} (\textbf{Robo}t \textbf{S}patial-\textbf{P}rocedural \textbf{A}ssessment), a large-scale robotic manipulation dataset and benchmark for diagnosing embodied reasoning in VLA models. \texttt{RoboSPA} focuses on two core dimensions, Fine-Grained Spatial Reasoning and Long-Horizon Procedural Planning, covering 10 task categories and 56 base tasks. Each task is instantiated across five difficulty levels, yielding 280 variants with increasing spatial ambiguity and procedural complexity. We collect 527K trajectories across multiple embodiments and diverse scenes. Beyond binary success rate, \texttt{RoboSPA} introduces diagnostic metrics for more detailed evaluation. Experiments on representative VLA models show that current systems still struggle with complex spatial relations, precise low-level execution, and memory-intensive planning. These results establish \texttt{RoboSPA} as a challenging diagnostic benchmark for developing more capable, reliable, and generalizable embodied agents. Our data and code are available at https://github.com/fanzhenxuan/RoboSPA.

</details>


### [214] [FIRE-LIVWO: Robust LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-Wheel Odometry via Failure-Immune mmWave Radar Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05325)
*Kun Hu,Menggang Li,Kaidi Wu,Zhiwen Jin,Yingjie Zhao,Chaoquan Tang,Eryi Hu,Gongbo Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FIRE-LIVWO，一种基于迭代误差状态卡尔曼滤波（IESKF）的多模态紧耦合里程计框架，融合4D毫米波雷达、LiDAR、视觉和轮式里程计信息，通过退化检测与自适应融合模型切换策略，有效应对煤矿井下烟雾遮挡和几何退化走廊等极端环境，实现了鲁棒且高精度的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型地下煤矿环境中，烟雾粉尘严重损失视觉信息和LiDAR点云特征，而长直自相似走廊导致几何退化，引发显著的里程计漂移，现有方法难以在复杂结构和严重退化条件下实现鲁棒SLAM。

Method: 构建基于IESKF的紧耦合多模态里程计框架，将4D毫米波雷达、LiDAR和视觉特征统一到VoxelMap中，联合构建LiDAR-radar点对面残差与稀疏视觉光度残差；烟雾环境下利用毫米波雷达穿透性并引入逐点多普勒速度约束维持状态可观测性；退化走廊中通过非完整约束（NHC）和在线杆臂补偿紧耦合轮式里程计；核心贡献是基于几何与视觉可观测性分析的退化检测和自适应融合模型切换策略。

Result: 在真实地下煤矿实验中，FIRE-LIVWO能够准确识别退化边界，实现极端条件下的可靠模态切换，平均定位误差达5.677m，相比基线方法在精度和鲁棒性上均表现更优，代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过可观测性量化分析与自适应模态权重调整，FIRE-LIVWO有效解决了煤矿井下烟雾与几何退化导致的定位失效问题，为复杂地下环境的多模态紧耦合SLAM提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Achieving robust SLAM in large-scale underground coal mines with complex structures and severe degeneracies remains highly challenging. Dense smoke and dust cause substantial loss of visual information and degrade LiDAR point-cloud features, while long, self-similar corridors induce geometric degeneration, leading to pronounced odometry drift. To address these issues, we propose FIRE-LIVWO: Failure-Immune mmWave Radar-Enhanced LiDAR-Inertial-Visual-Wheel Odometry, a tightly coupled multi-modal odometry framework based on an iterated error-state Kalman filter (IESKF). The framework fuses 4D mmWave radar, LiDAR, and visual features within a unified VoxelMap and jointly constructs LiDAR-radar point-to-plane residuals and sparse visual photometric residuals. In smoke-filled environments, we exploit the strong penetration of 4D mmWave radar and introduce pointwise Doppler velocity constraints to preserve state observability. In geometrically degenerate corridors, we tightly couple wheel odometry using non-holonomic constraints (NHC) and online lever-arm compensation to reduce drift. Our central contribution is a degeneration detection and adaptive fusion model switching strategy grounded in geometric and visual observability analysis, which quantifies observability online and dynamically adjusts modality weights. Real-world experiments in underground coal mines demonstrate that FIRE-LIVWO accurately identifies failure boundaries, enabling reliable modality switching under extreme conditions. Compared with baselines, it achieves superior accuracy and robustness (average localization error of 5.677m). We open source our code on Github to benefit the robotics community.

</details>


### [215] [Adaptation Needs in Robotic Systems: Assessing Behavior Trees and Their Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05331)
*Mehran Rostamnia,Gianluca Filippone,Ricardo Caldas,Patrizio Pelliccione*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过文献驱动研究结合实证验证，系统分析了行为树（BTs）在满足现代机器人自适应需求方面的充分性，将自适应需求分类为六类（知识、感知、执行、系统、任务、环境），并将基于BT的自适应方法归纳为四大家族（生成、扩展、演化、精化），最终指出经典BT在运行时重构、不确定性推理、任务重解释、学习和外部知识集成等方面存在不足，并提出了何时使用经典BT、何时需要增强机制以及面临的挑战的指导性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器人在动态、不确定和开放环境中运行，设计时的假设可能不再成立，机器人系统需要自适应能力。行为树因其模块化、可读性和响应性被广泛使用，但经典行为树是否足以满足现代机器人系统的自适应需求仍然是一个未解决的核心问题。

Method: 采用文献驱动研究方法：首先从现有文献中提炼出机器人自适应需求的分类体系（六类）；然后分析经典行为树在这些需求方面的能力与局限；随后梳理现有基于行为树的自适应方法，将其归纳为四个主要家族（生成、扩展、演化、精化），并辅以实证验证。

Result: 分析表明，经典行为树的模块性、灵活性和响应性不足以应对涉及运行时重构、不确定性推理、任务重解释、学习以及与外部知识和规划机制集成的自适应需求。增强型行为树方法虽能解决部分局限，但覆盖程度不同且自身也存在局限。研究将自适应需求与经典和增强行为树的能力/局限相关联，提供了选择指导。

Conclusion: 研究结论为自适应机器人控制架构提供了实用指导：明确在何种条件下经典行为树足够使用，何时需要引入增强机制，并指出了自适应机器人控制架构中仍存在或新出现的挑战。

Abstract: Robotic systems increasingly operate in dynamic, uncertain, and open-ended environments, where design-time assumptions may no longer hold, and adaptation becomes necessary to maintain effective and safe operation. Behavior Trees (BTs) are widely used in robotic control architectures due to their modularity, readability, and reactivity. This raises a central question: are BTs sufficient to meet the adaptation needs of modern robotic systems?
  This paper investigates this question through a literature-driven study complemented by empirical validation. First, we derive a classification of robotic adaptation needs from the literature, organizing them into six categories: Knowledge, Perception, Actuation, System, Mission, and Environment. Then, we analyze the capabilities and limitations of classical BTs with respect to these needs. Then, we characterize BT-based approaches for adaptation from the existing literature and organize them into four primary families, i.e., generation, extension, evolution, and refinement, including approaches that combine multiple families.
  Our analysis shows that the modularity, flexibility, and reactivity of classical BTs are insufficient for adaptation needs involving runtime restructuring, reasoning under uncertainty, mission reinterpretation, learning, or integration with external knowledge and planning mechanisms. Enhanced BT approaches address several of these limitations, but to different extents and often with limitations of their own. Our findings relate adaptation needs to both the capabilities and limitations of classical and enhanced BTs, providing guidance on when classical BTs are sufficient, when enhanced mechanisms are needed, and which challenges remain or emerge for adaptive robotic control architectures.

</details>


### [216] [Development of a Humanoid Robot Prototype for Multimodal Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05361)
*Thang Tran Viet,Thanh Nguyen Canh,Huy Uong Gia,Phuc Dinh Van,Son Tran Duc,Ngoc Minh Do,Xiem HoangVan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款作为人机交互（HRI）测试平台的人形机器人原型，配备12自由度双臂、2自由度表情头部、定制控制器和Jetson AI处理模块，集成了手势识别、物体检测与3D定位、语音命令处理三大AI模块。实验验证定位精度约1.83cm，任务准确率超90%，手势识别96%，语音识别92%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HRI系统在开发与集成AI模块时缺乏灵活、可复现的测试平台，作者旨在构建一个开放、低成本、模块化的人形机器人平台，以支持HRI研究中的AI算法验证与原型开发。

Method: 设计了一款12自由度双臂+2自由度头部（带LCD表情屏）的人形机器人，采用定制控制器板与板载Jetson进行实时AI处理，集成了三大AI模块：基于MediaPipe Pose与LSTM的手势识别、基于YOLO与3D定位的物体检测、以及基于语音识别与LLM语义解析的语音指令处理。

Result: 定位实验中平均操作误差约1.83cm；整体任务准确率超过90%，其中手势识别准确率达96%，语音识别准确率达92%，验证了系统的有效性与多功能性。

Conclusion: 该人形机器人平台被证实为一种可复现、易获取的HRI研究原型系统，能够有效支持AI模块的集成与验证，适合推广用于HRI领域的学术研究与快速原型开发。

Abstract: Human-robot interaction (HRI) enables intuitive and intelligent collaboration between humans and robots in real-world environments. This paper introduces a humanoid robot prototype designed as a flexible testbed for developing and integrating artificial intelligence (AI) modules in HRI tasks. The system features a 12 degree-of-freedom (DOFs) dual-arm mechanism and a 2 DOFs head with an expressive LCD screen to express facial emotions. All hardware components are controlled by a custom-designed controller board with real-time AI processing supported by an onboard Jetson module. The system incorporates three AI modules: (1) gesture recognition using MediaPipe Pose and an LSTM classifier, (2) object detection with YOLO and 3D localization, and (3) voice-command processing through speech recognition and large language model(LLM)-based semantic parsing. The platform is validated through experiments on positioning accuracy, with results showing average manipulation errors of approximately 1.83 cm. To demonstrate its versatility, experimental results show over 90% task accuracy, with gesture recognition reaching 96%, speech recognition reaching 92%. The results confirm the effectiveness of the proposed system as a reproducible and accessible humanoid platform for research and prototyping in HRI.

</details>


### [217] [Towards Neuro-Symbolic Procedural Reasoning for Long-Horizon Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05369)
*Vivek Chavan,Yahuan Shi,Oliver Heimann,Kevin Haninger,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号框架，将VLA模型（视觉-语言-动作）与显式任务图和多模态程序记忆相结合，以增强长程操作任务的可靠性。任务图编码动作依赖和分支条件，记忆维护活动步骤与视觉证据；同时利用遥操作视频中标注的伪注视作为人类空间/时间引导信号用于VLA微调与推理，在两种长程操作域（工作区清理和手术器械操作）中验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在短操作技能上表现良好，但在需要持久任务状态、依赖感知推理、条件决策和可靠接地（grounding）的长程程序中仍然脆弱，缺乏结构化的任务规划能力和可靠的视觉引导。

Method: 构建神经符号框架：显式任务图编码动作依赖、有效转换和分支条件；多模态程序记忆维护活动步骤、已完成任务、文本上下文和任务相关视觉证据；任务图与记忆共同指导对象选择、目的地接地、子目标调度及状态转换验证。初始研究绕过跨视角注视迁移，直接在机器人视角遥操作视频中标注伪注视，用于VLA微调与推理阶段。

Result: 在两种长程操作域（工作区清理和手术器械操作）中评估了正确对象/目的地选择、子任务完成度、任务进展、步骤顺序一致性、完整任务成功率和程序/执行错误等指标，验证了结构化符号推理与演示衍生的视觉引导的互补效果。

Conclusion: 结构化符号推理与演示衍生的视觉引导可作为互补机制，显著提升长程VLA操作的可靠性和完整性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can execute short manipulation skills, but remain brittle in long-horizon procedures requiring persistent task state, dependency-aware reasoning, conditional decisions, and reliable grounding. We investigate a neuro-symbolic framework that combines learned VLA control with explicit task graphs and multimodal procedural memory. Task graphs encode action dependencies, valid transitions, and branch conditions, while memory maintains the active step, completed actions, textual context, and task-relevant visual evidence. Together, these structures guide object selection, destination grounding, subgoal dispatch, and verification of expected state transitions. Human demonstrations provide additional spatial and temporal guidance through gaze or saliency cues. To isolate their effect on policy learning, our initial study bypasses cross-view gaze transfer and directly annotates pseudo-gaze in robot-view teleoperation videos. The resulting guidance is used during VLA fine-tuning and inference. We study two long-horizon manipulation domains, workspace clearing and surgical-instrument handling, which require ordered execution, visually grounded decisions, and conditional branching. We evaluate correct-object and destination selection, subtask completion, task progress, step-order consistency, complete-task success, and procedural or execution mistakes. This work positions structured symbolic reasoning and demonstration-derived visual guidance as complementary mechanisms for reliable long-horizon VLA manipulation.

</details>


### [218] [What Matters, When? Diagnosing and Improving Conditional Visual Grounding in Visuomotor Imitation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05376)
*Vivek Chavan,Pengtao Xie,Yahuan Shi,Oliver Heimann,Kevin Haninger,Jörg Krüger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了视动模仿策略在视觉干扰物存在下的失败机理，将其归因于条件性视觉接地问题（视觉目标随操作阶段和任务状态变化），并提出了三种互补干预方法（干扰物增强、阶段相关注意力正则化、外观视觉提示）来改善目标选择，在仿真和物理UR3e机器人上均显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视动模仿策略在训练分布内视觉条件下表现良好，但当引入视觉上相似的干扰物体或容器时容易失败，现有研究对这一失败模式缺乏系统性的理解和解决方案。

Method: 首先使用Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT)系统引入具有受控颜色和形状相似度的干扰物，将失败定位到拾取和放置阶段；基于诊断结果，提出三种干预方法：干扰物增强、阶段相关的注意力正则化和外观视觉提示。

Result: 实验表明干扰物敏感性取决于干扰物视觉相似类型和操作阶段；提出的三种干预方法在仿真和物理UR3e上显著提升鲁棒性，并且在预训练的视觉-语言-行动策略的状态条件仪器操作任务中也观察到并缓解了类似的失败模式。

Conclusion: 视觉干扰物即使在底层操作技能不受损的情况下也可能导致错误的物体或目标位置选择，显式改善目标选择可以跨不同的视动策略学习范式大幅恢复性能。

Abstract: Visuomotor imitation policies can achieve high performance under in-distribution visual conditions yet fail when visually similar objects or receptacles are introduced. We study this behavior as a problem of conditional visual grounding: the visual target required for successful control changes with the manipulation phase and, in more complex tasks, with the observed task state. Using Action Chunking with Transformers (ACT), we systematically introduce distractor objects and receptacles with controlled color and shape similarity and localize failures to picking and placement. We find that distractor sensitivity is specific to both the type of visual similarity and the manipulation stage. Guided by this diagnosis, we evaluate distractor augmentation, phase-dependent attention regularization, and appearance-based visual prompting as complementary interventions for improving target selection while preserving spatial information required for control. These interventions substantially improve robustness in simulation and on a physical UR3e. We further examine the same failure pattern in a pretrained vision-language-action policy on a state-conditioned instrument-handling task, where the observed state of a medical instrument determines the correct destination. Together, the results show that visual distractors can cause incorrect object or destination selection even when the underlying manipulation skill remains intact, and that explicitly improving target selection can substantially recover performance across distinct visuomotor policy-learning regimes.

</details>


### [219] [Same Trajectory, Contradictory Rewards (ROBORMBENCH): Paraphrase Fragility in Vision Language Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05401)
*Wonje Jeung,Sangyeon Yoon,Hyesoo Hong,Yoonjun Cho,Dongjae Jeon,Bumjun Kim,Jean Oh,Youngjae Yu,Albert No*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出现有视觉-语言模型（VLM）作为机器人学习奖励函数时缺乏释义不变性，即同义指令描述下同一轨迹应获得相同奖励，但实际中释义变化会显著改变预测得分甚至翻转成败判定。作者提出ROBORMBENCH基准（包含2,390条真实机器人轨迹、真实进度标签和21,673个已验证释义），发现开源与专有VLM均普遍存在严重的释义不稳定问题，且该问题随改写差异增大而加剧，无法通过模型规模或显式推理可靠缓解，但专门训练的奖励模型更为稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM被广泛用作机器人学习的奖励函数，但其必须具备释义不变性（paraphrase invariance）这一核心属性——语义等价的目标描述应对同一轨迹给出相同奖励。然而现有研究未系统性验证该属性，作者旨在揭示并量化这一被忽视的失效模式。

Method: 构建ROBORMBENCH基准测试集，包含2,390条真实机器人轨迹、地面真值进度标签及21,673条经核验的释义，覆盖词汇、句法和动作目标改写三种类型。在多个专有和开源VLM上进行系统评测。

Result: 释义引起的预测不稳定问题在各类VLM中普遍且严重；改写差异越大，不稳定程度越高；增大模型规模或引入显式推理并不能可靠地缓解该问题；而使用轨迹监督训练的专用奖励模型显著更稳定。

Conclusion: 释义鲁棒性是VLM用于机器人奖励建模的核心要求，仅追求模型规模或推理能力不足以解决该问题，需要专门的稳定性设计与轨迹级监督训练。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used as reward functions for robotic learning, but this role requires paraphrase invariance: the same trajectory should receive the same reward under semantically equivalent goal descriptions. We show that current VLM reward models often violate this property. Paraphrasing the instruction alone can substantially change predicted progress scores, and can even flip identical robot behavior between failure and success. To measure this failure mode, we introduce ROBORMBENCH, a benchmark with 2,390 real-robot trajectories, ground-truth progress labels, and 21,673 verified paraphrases spanning lexical, syntactic, and action-goal rewrites. Across proprietary and open-source VLMs, paraphrase-induced instability is widespread and severe, grows under more divergent rewrites, and is not reliably reduced by scale or explicit reasoning. Dedicated reward models trained with trajectory-grounded supervision are substantially more stable. These results show that paraphrase robustness is a core requirement for reliable VLM-based reward modeling in robotics.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [220] [Spectral-Target Physical Latent Structuring for JEPA-Style World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04264)
*Penghao Zhu,Salvatore Penachio,Kaustav Mukherjee,Aneesh Jonelagadda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对潜在世界模型（如LeWorldModel）在高动态环境中出现的"物理表征懒惰"问题，提出了一种训练时辅助监督方法——轻量级"傅里叶辅助头"，在不增加推理成本的前提下强制潜在空间具备物理信息结构，显著提升了规划成功率和数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在世界模型即使通过正则化（如SIGReg）避免了表征坍缩，仍然存在一种新发现的失败模式——"物理表征懒惰"：潜在状态未坍缩但未能表示关键物理属性，导致下游规划任务普遍失败。这一问题在高动态环境中尤为突出。

Method: 提出在训练阶段引入轻量级"傅里叶辅助头"进行辅助监督，对潜在空间施加物理信息结构约束。该方法无额外推理成本，可推广至任意环境。

Result: 实验表明该方法在高动态环境中显著提升规划成功率（基线LeWM存在物理表征懒惰的场景）；在无懒惰的其他环境中也有适度改进。规划性能的提升伴随潜在空间与关键物理属性相关性的增强。在低数据条件下，辅助监督对成功率的提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: 傅里叶辅助头方法能有效改善潜在世界模型的规划成功率与数据效率，避免物理表征懒惰问题，支持学习到的表征具备物理结构化特性有助于提升规划性能的假设。

Abstract: Latent world models have become increasingly popular as a method to predict and plan in latent space rather than pixel space. Recent architectures, such as LeWorldModel (LeWM), jointly train the encoder and predictor using regularization techniques like SIGReg to prevent representation collapse. Even with such regularization preventing representation collapse, we identify a new world model failure mode of \textit{physical representation laziness}, particularly noted in highly dynamic environments. For these lazy cases, the learned latent states do not collapse but nonetheless fail to represent key physical properties, causing ubiquitous downstream planning failure. To resolve this issue, we propose training-time auxiliary supervision with a lightweight "Fourier auxiliary head", which enforces physically-informed structuring of the latent space with no additional inference-time cost and can be generalized to any environment. Experimentally, we show that the auxiliary head substantially improves planning success rates in dynamic environments where the baseline LeWM exhibits physical representation laziness. It also leads to modest improvements in other environments, even when the baseline does not exhibit physical representation laziness. We further observe superior planning performance being accompanied by higher latent space correlations with key physical properties, indicating both the ability of our method to physically structure latent states and the potential planning-side benefit to the learned representation being physically structured. We also see in low-data regimes, auxiliary supervision is particularly impactful in increasing success rate. These findings support the use of our Fourier auxiliary head method to improve both overall success rate and data efficiency, while avoiding representation laziness in latent world models.

</details>


### [221] [ProToMEx: Rapid, Interpretable Explanations via Structured Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04265)
*Athina Georgara,Adarsh Valoor,Sarvapali D. Ramchurn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProToMEx，一种基于概率主题模型的模型无关可解释性框架，通过学习潜在"主题"来揭示分类背后的高阶语义结构，而非仅仅提供特征重要性。该方法同时提供全局和局部解释，且局部解释生成速度比SHAP和LIME快约30-40倍，同时保持相当的保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的后验可解释方法（如SHAP、LIME）主要关注特征归因，即为单个特征分配重要性分数，但难以表达驱动模型决策的复杂组合模式。这促使作者探索一种新的可解释性范式，超越简单特征重要性以揭示底层语义结构。

Method: ProToMEx是一个模型无关框架，利用概率主题模型（PTMs）学习潜在"主题"，每个主题代表一个分类的不同高层次原因。该框架天然支持两类解释：全局解释（模型整体行为）和局部解释（可解缠某一预测的多个共存原因），并以低摊销计算成本生成局部解释。

Result: 在标准表格数据集和合成数据集上的实验表明，ProToMEx生成的解释在保真度上与SHAP和LIME相当，但生成局部解释的摊销计算成本大幅降低——比SHAP和LIME快约30-40倍，适合实时应用场景。

Conclusion: ProToMEx提供了一种超越传统特征归因的新颖可解释性范式，在保持解释质量的同时显著提升计算效率，展示了概率主题模型在机器学习可解释性领域的应用潜力，特别适用于对实时性有要求的场景。

Abstract: Existing post-hoc explainers for machine learning classifiers primarily focus on feature attribution, assigning importance scores to individual features. While valuable, this approach struggles to articulate the complex, combinatorial patterns that often drive a model's decision-making process. To overcome this limitation, we introduce ProToMEx, a new paradigm for explainability that leverages Probabilistic Topic Models (PTMs). Our model-agnostic framework learns latent ''topics'' that represent distinct, high-level reasons for a classification, moving beyond simple feature importance to reveal underlying semantic structures. ProToMEx naturally provides both global explanations of a model's overall behaviour and local explanations that can disentangle multiple co-existing reasons for a specific prediction. We demonstrate empirically that ProToMEx not only produces explanations of comparable fidelity to popular methods like SHAP and LIME but also drastically reduces the amortised computational cost of generating local explanations, making it highly suitable for real-time applications. Specifically, we show that ProToMEx is ~30-40x faster than SHAP and LIME over standardised tabular datasets and synthetic datasets.

</details>


### [222] [A Data Fusion Framework for Grounding Aerospace Surrogate Model via Experimental Wind-Tunnel Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04267)
*Nitin Nagesh Kulkarni,Dheeraj Vemula,Yin Yu,Peter Lyu,Juan J. Alonso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种实验校正框架，通过风洞PSP测量数据来校正基于CFD训练的深度学习代理模型，在不重新训练代理模型的前提下学习CFD与实验之间的系统性偏差，从而大幅提升代理模型在NASA CRM机翼-机身构型上的表面压力分布预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真CFD训练的代理模型虽然能精确复现CFD的数值预测，但CFD本身与实验观测之间存在系统性差异，导致代理模型在实际工程应用中的预测保真度受限。现有方法需要重新训练代理模型来融合实验数据，计算成本高昂且可能损害模型泛化能力。

Method: 采用Geotransolver代理模型（基于2300个高保真CFD仿真训练，覆盖几何形变、马赫数0.70-0.85和攻角0-4度），在无需重训练的前提下，额外训练一个校正网络。该校正网络基于两个马赫数（0.70和0.85）下空间配准的PSP测量数据，学习代理模型预测与实验测量表面压力分布之间的差异。

Result: 在马赫数0.85下，校正后模型显著改善了与PSP测量的一致性，特别是在吸力峰、激波位置和压力恢复区域。在保留的攻角测试中，校正后的代理模型与测量的差异在2.3%-2.7%的Cp范围内，且在所有测试状态下均优于测量条件之间的直接插值方法。同时减少了预测误差大小以及误差超过0.05 Cp的湿表面比例。

Conclusion: 实验测量能够通过学习系统性的CFD-实验差异来校正大规模的仿真训练代理模型，同时保留其泛化能力和计算效率。这项工作为在有限的实验数据条件下提升高保真CFD代理模型的实际预测精度提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Aerodynamic surrogate models trained on high-fidelity CFD data reproduce numerical predictions of both scalar outputs and entire fields accurately, yet their predictive fidelity is limited by systematic discrepancies between CFD and experimental observations. We present an experimentally grounded correction framework that adapts a CFD-trained deep learning surrogate using wind-tunnel PSP measurements. A Geotransolver surrogate trained on 2,300 high-fidelity CFD simulations of the NASA CRM wing-body configuration, spanning geometric variation, Mach 0.70-0.85, and angles of attack 0 to 4 degrees, reproduces the CFD integrated aerodynamic forces and pitching moment to R2 > 0.99 but does not match the experimental data. To incorporate experimental information without retraining the surrogate, a correction network is trained on spatially registered PSP measurements at two freestream Mach numbers (0.70 and 0.85) across the same angle-of-attack range, learning the discrepancy between the surrogate-predicted and experimentally measured surface-pressure distributions. At Mach 0.85 the correction substantially improves agreement with PSP, particularly at the wing suction peak, shock location, and subsequent pressure recovery, reducing both the magnitude of the prediction error and the fraction of wetted surface on which it exceeds 0.05 in Cp, and it does so from a limited experimental dataset without modifying the pretrained surrogate parameters. On held-out angles of attack the grounded surrogate agrees with measurement to within 2.3-2.7% of the measured Cp range, and outperforms direct interpolation between the measured conditions at every state tested. Experimental measurements can therefore ground a large-scale simulation-trained surrogate by learning systematic CFD-to-experiment discrepancies while preserving its generalization capability and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [223] [Quantum-Assisted Memory-Efficient Training for Parameter-Intensive Wi-Fi-Based Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04271)
*To Truong An,Jie Zhang,Guolin Yin,Junqing Zhang,Yanjiao Li,Trung Q. Duong,Simon L. Cotton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-MET框架，利用混合量子-经典神经网络间接生成Wi-Fi人体活动识别（HAR）模型参数，结合训练阶段的结构化剪枝，在减少90%-95%可训练参数的同时保持甚至超越经典分类精度，并实现75%-85%的模型稀疏度且分类精度损失小于2%，是首个同时解决HAR系统训练和推理阶段内存效率问题的量子辅助方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Wi-Fi的HAR系统依赖深度学习模型，在训练和推理阶段计算和内存开销巨大，数百万参数的同步更新导致内存消耗过高，难以在资源受限设备上实际部署。

Method: 提出Q-MET框架，采用混合量子-经典神经网络间接生成HAR模型参数以减少可训练参数量，并在训练阶段集成结构化剪枝以支持轻量级推理。

Result: 与传统反向传播深度学习训练相比，可训练参数减少90%-95%，同时保持或超过经典分类精度；通过结构化剪枝实现75%-85%的模型稀疏度，分类精度损失小于2%。

Conclusion: Q-MET是首个量子辅助的HAR方法，能够同时解决训练和推理两个阶段的内存效率瓶颈，为在资源受限环境中部署Wi-Fi HAR系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Wi-Fi-based human activity recognition (HAR) has become an important part of integrated sensing and communications, paving the way for a range of context-aware services. However, most existing Wi-Fi-based HAR systems rely on deep learning (DL) models that are computationally and memory intensive in both training and inference, which poses significant challenges for real-world deployment. Conventional training requires simultaneous updates of millions of parameters, leading to prohibitive memory consumption. In this paper, we propose a novel quantum-assisted memory-efficient training framework (Q-MET) designed to improve efficiency in both training and inference. Q-MET utilizes a hybrid quantum classical neural network to indirectly generate parameters for HAR models, significantly reducing the trainable parameter count compared to direct optimization. To further support the deployment on resource-constrained devices, we integrate structured pruning during the training phase. Experimental results demonstrate that Q-MET achieves a 90% to 95% reduction in trainable parameters compared with conventional backpropagation-based DL training while maintaining or even exceeding classical classification accuracy. Additionally, Q-MET supports lightweight inference through structured pruning, achieving 75% to 85% model sparsity with less than 2% loss in classification accuracy. To the best of our knowledge, this work represents the first quantum-assisted approach to simultaneously tackle memory inefficiencies in both the training and inference stages of HAR systems.

</details>


### [224] [Evaluating Large Language Models for Forced Outage Risk Prediction: Benefits and Comparison to Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04272)
*Christos Petridis,Zoran Obradovic,Mladen Kezunovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型在零样本框架下预测配电网天气相关强迫停电风险的能力，与监督学习模型进行对比评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需标注训练数据即可利用LLM进行天气相关配电网停电风险预测的可行性，以弥补传统监督方法对标注数据依赖的不足。

Method: 将问题构建为三组预测时段（3h/6h/12h）的二元严重程度分类任务，使用六年停电记录和高分辨率天气数据，在两种输入配置（当前天气观测与天气预报数据）下，对四个零样本LLM与两个监督分类器进行基准对比测试。

Result: 监督模型在宏F1和精确率方面优于LLM，而新一代LLM取得了具有竞争力的分数。此外，LLM在可操作推理和地理可扩展性方面展现出互补优势。

Conclusion: 将LLM与监督模型相结合可能是在停电风险预测中的最佳实践方案。

Abstract: This study examines the ability of large language models (LLMs) to predict the risk of weather-related forced outages in the distribution grid in a zero-shot framework, without labeled training data. The problem is formulated as a binary severity classification task across three forecast horizons (3h, 6h, 12h), using six years of outage records and high-resolution weather data for a utility service area in central Texas. Four zero-shot LLMs are benchmarked against two supervised classifiers across two input configurations: one using current weather observations and the other using weather forecast data. Results show that supervised models outperform LLMs on macro-F1 and precision, while newer LLM generations achieve competitive scores. Beyond accuracy, LLMs offer complementary strengths in actionable reasoning and geographic scalability, suggesting that combining them with supervised models may be the best practice.

</details>


### [225] [BER-PEF: Unified Human Mobility Predictability Evaluation via Bayes Error Rate Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04292)
*En Xu,Jingtao Ding,Zhiwen Yu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BER-PEF框架，将贝叶斯错误率（BER）估计转化为移动性可预测性估计，并在缺乏可观测真实值的情况下，为不同可预测性估计器提供统一的比较协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类移动性可预测性的真实值在真实移动数据上不可直接观测，现有可比协议不统一，难以在不同估计器之间进行公平验证和比较。

Method: BER-PEF框架将符号序列、数值轨迹、上下文特征和学习表示映射到统一的特征-标签空间，通过受控扰动曲线评估估计器输出，并以与共享可预测性参考区间的偏差（区间下偏差、区间上偏差、全区间偏差）作为度量标准。

Result: 在Foursquare NYC和TKY、GeoLife、T-Drive上的实验表明，多个基于BER的估计器在符号序列和数值轨迹上的参考偏差低于现有可预测性方法，其估计值能跟踪扰动力下的经验预测性能变化；上下文输入和多结构表示可在同一协议下评估，多扰动级别的证据聚合比单次未扰动观测更可靠。

Conclusion: BER-PEF为异构移动数据上的可预测性估计器提供了一种统一且可验证的评估路径，适用于真实可预测性不可获取的场景。

Abstract: Human mobility predictability concerns the best prediction performance attainable from a given target and input information, but its ground truth is not directly observable on real mobility data. We present BER-PEF, a Bayes-error-rate-based framework that converts BER estimation into mobility predictability estimation and provides a unified protocol for comparing estimators without observable ground truth. The framework maps symbolic sequences, numeric trajectories, contextual features, and learned representations into a common feature--label space, then evaluates estimator outputs along controlled perturbation curves against a shared predictability reference interval by measuring deviations below the interval, above the interval, and across the full interval. Experiments on Foursquare NYC and TKY, GeoLife, and T-Drive show that several BER-based estimators achieve lower reference discrepancy than existing predictability methods on symbolic sequences and numeric trajectories, while their estimates track changes in empirical prediction performance under perturbation. Additional analyses show that contextual inputs and multiple structured representations can be evaluated under the same protocol, and that aggregating evidence across multiple perturbation levels provides a more reliable basis for estimator selection than relying on a single unperturbed observation. BER-PEF therefore offers a unified and verifiable path for evaluating predictability estimators on heterogeneous mobility data when ground-truth predictability is unavailable.

</details>


### [226] [Data-Driven Learning of Unknown Nonlinear Differential Equations Using Functional Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04329)
*Seyyed Shaho Alaviani,Yongzhi Qu,Gregory W. Vogl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的可解释机器学习方法，基于泛函分析和算子理论，将非线性常微分方程的数据驱动发现问题重新表述。该方法无需系统物理先验知识，仅从单条状态轨迹数据中学习未知向量场，采用函数空间中的积分距离作为代价函数，并通过增量学习算法实现在线学习，可同时处理受迫/无受迫、自治/非自治动力系统并发现未知外力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据驱动ODE发现方法主要基于离散误差求和构建代价函数，缺乏严格的理论基础，且难以同时处理非自治系统和未知外力情形，需要更具可解释性的新方法。

Method: 基于泛函分析与算子理论对问题重新表述；在函数空间中构造两个函数间的积分距离作为代价函数（替代离散误差和）；提出增量学习算法以在线方式处理新训练样本。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法能够同时发现随时间变化的外力项和未知底层动力学，适用于受迫/无受迫、自治/非自治（时变）动力系统。

Conclusion: 该方法在数据驱动ODE发现方面具有理论严谨性和可解释性，且能在线增量学习，相较于现有方法具有明显优势。

Abstract: In this paper, the problem of data-driven discovery of nonlinear ordinary differential equations (ODEs) is recast, and a new interpretable machine learning (ML) method is proposed. The proposed method aims to learn the unknown vector field of nonlinear dynamics without prior knowledge of the system's physics from only one single state trajectory's data. The proposed method has two fundamental differences with existing methods: 1) the formulation presented in this method is derived based on Functional Analysis and Operator Theory, and 2) the cost function is constructed in the function space as a distance between two functions as an integral, instead of the discrete-sum of errors used in existing ML approaches. An incremental learning algorithm is proposed to learn the unknown vector field to handle new training samples in an online manner. The proposed method can discover the unknown vector field from both forced and unforced autonomous and non-autonomous (or time-varying) dynamical systems. The proposed method is able to simultaneously discover unknown external forces as a function of time and unknown underlying dynamics. Finally, numerical examples are given to demonstrate the advantages of the proposed method.

</details>


### [227] [Modular Deep Recurrent Neural Network: Application to Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04339)
*Nima Mohajerin,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种模块化深度循环神经网络（RNN），通过增加前馈层间连接来改善多层RNN对高阶动力学和非线性的学习能力，并缓解空间维度上的梯度消失/爆炸问题，在四旋翼飞行器高度动力学建模中验证了其优于现有方法的泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RNN架构部署过程繁琐，且多层RNN在学习和建模高阶动力学与非线性时能力有限，同时存在空间维度上的梯度消失/爆炸问题，导致难以有效训练深度RNN模型。

Method: 提出一种模块化深度RNN框架，能够灵活部署各种RNN架构并自动计算梯度。该框架衍生出一类包含前馈层间连接的新架构，通过在多层RNN各层之间添加前馈连接来改善信息流动。

Result: 实验表明，增加前馈层间连接的RNN在学习高阶动力学和非线性方面的能力显著提升，且空间维度上的梯度消失/爆炸问题得到缓解。在四旋翼飞行器海拔动力学建模中，该方法能够有效学习模型，而现有方法泛化速度慢或根本无法泛化。

Conclusion: 前馈层间连接是多层RNN中的一种有效架构改进，能够提升深度RNN的学习能力与泛化性能，为解决空间梯度问题提供了新思路。模块化设计也使RNN架构的部署和梯度计算更加便捷。

Abstract: A modular deep Recurrent Neural Network (RNN) is introduced to facilitate the process of deploying various architectures of RNNs, and to automatically compute derivatives for gradient-based learning methods. The modularity leads to a set of new architectures, one of which includes feedforward inter-layer connections. By adding feedforward inter-layer connections in a multi-layer RNN, it is observed that the capability of the RNN to learn and model high-order dynamics and nonlinearities is significantly improved. The problem of vanishing/exploding gradient in space for a multilayer RNN is also alleviated using feedforward connections. These results are demonstrated using a quadrotor case study, for which a model of the altitude dynamics is learned with our particular network structure, while existing methods are unable to generalize as quickly or at all.

</details>


### [228] [SharedSAE: One Feature Dictionary Across Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04344)
*Daniil Ognev,Célian Vasson,Lijie Hu,Kentaro Inui,Benjamin Heinzerling*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SharedSAE方法，用一个共享稀疏自编码器替代多个模型各自专用的SAE，在四个不同家族的1B规模语言模型上保持96.6%的解释方差，且潜变量描述可跨模型迁移，新模型可用共享词典快速适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SAE训练和潜变量标注需要对每个模型重复进行，成本高昂且缺乏可复用性。作者希望建立单个共享SAE来统一解释多个模型，避免为每个模型单独训练和标注。

Method: SharedSAE结合了共享词典与模型特定的编码器-解码器对。与先前方法不同，它仅归一化选择分数而非丢弃激活幅度，从而保留幅度信息；并采用模型丢弃机制支持单模型推理。

Result: 在四个不同家族的1B规模基础语言模型上，SharedSAE保留了专用SAE平均解释方差的96.6%；其潜变量激活的跨模型相关性比事后对齐的独立SAE高1.8倍；潜变量描述可跨模型迁移；词典冻结后新模型可高效适配并接近专用SAE的重建质量。

Conclusion: 单个共享SAE可以替代多个专用SAE集合，大幅节省训练和标注成本，同时保持较高的解释质量和跨模型可迁移性，为多模型可解释性提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret language model activations, but SAE training and latent labelling are typically repeated for every model. Here, we show that a single shared SAE can replace a collection of dedicated per-model SAEs. Our method, SharedSAE, combines a shared dictionary with model-specific encoder-decoder pairs. Unlike the closest prior method, which discards activation magnitudes and requires all models at inference, SharedSAE instead normalizes only selection scores, preserving magnitudes, and uses model dropout for single-model inference. We train SharedSAE on four 1B-scale base language models spanning distinct families and tokenizers. Despite sharing its latents across models, SharedSAE retains 96.6% of dedicated SAEs' mean explained variance; its latent activations exhibit cross-model correlations 1.8 times as high as separate SAEs aligned post-hoc, and its latent descriptions transfer across models. After the dictionary is frozen, new models can be efficiently adapted to it, achieving near-dedicated-SAE reconstruction quality while reusing the shared latent descriptions.

</details>


### [229] [A Quantum Variational Approach to Prototypical Recurrent Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04354)
*Mahyar Sadeghi Garjan,Tommaso Cesari,Michel Barbeau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的量子原型循环单元（QPRU），其参数量显著少于经典循环架构（LSTM、GRU）以及量子变体（QLSTM、QGRU）。尽管设计紧凑，QPRU在预测性能上仍能媲美最先进的基线模型，同时具有更好的可扩展性和更少的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子循环架构如QLSTM和QGRU虽然在经典模型基础上引入量子计算，但仍存在参数量过大、可扩展性不足的问题，亟需一种更轻量化的量子循环单元以降低资源需求。

Method: 提出了一种轻量级量子原型循环单元（QPRU），通过紧凑的结构设计大幅减少可训练参数数量，同时保持循环网络的时间序列建模能力。

Result: QPRU在预测任务上取得了与最先进基线相当的竞争力表现，同时参数量显著少于经典LSTM、GRU及量子QLSTM、QGRU等变体。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了轻量化量子循环架构的可行性，在保证预测性能的同时提供了更好的可扩展性和更低的参数开销，展示了量子计算在资源受限场景下应用于时间序列预测的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce a lightweight Quantum Prototypical Recurrent Unit (QPRU) that requires significantly fewer parameters than both classical recurrent architectures, such as Long Short- Term Memory (LSTM) and Gated Recurrent Unit (GRU), and quantum variants, including Quantum LSTM (QLSTM) and Quantum GRU (QGRU). Despite its compact design, the QPRU achieves competitive forecasting performance, matching state-of-the-art baselines while offering important structural and practical advantages, including enhanced scalability and a reduced number of trainable parameters.

</details>


### [230] [On the Abundance of Critical Points of the t-SNE Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04379)
*Nakul Haridas,Ryan Murray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从能量景观角度分析t-SNE算法，针对其非凸性导致的复杂优化问题，利用原始特征空间与目标嵌入空间中的成对离散对称性，构造了无限族不同的临界点，用以解释t-SNE在实践中出现的拓扑破坏与虚假聚类等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: t-SNE算法虽被广泛使用，但其能量的非凸性使其难以被严格理解，现有多项数值实验表明其存在大量不尊重数据拓扑或聚类结构的局部极小值，作者希望为这些现象提供严格的数学解释。

Method: 针对包含原始t-SNE及大数据极限在内的一般能量族，假设特征空间密度满足连续对称性，通过识别原始特征空间与目标嵌入空间中在梯度动力学下保持不变的成对离散对称性，构造无穷多族不同的临界点，并辅以数值与分析实例进行验证。

Result: 成功构造了无限族具有不同特征的临界点，这些临界构型表现出拓扑破坏和虚假聚类等实证中常见的行为，并通过多个数值示例加以说明。

Conclusion: 该工作为理解t-SNE复杂能量景观提供了严格的理论第一步，揭示了离散对称性配对是产生虚假聚类与拓扑失真等现象的内在机制。

Abstract: This paper considers the energy landscape of the t-SNE algorithm. While this algorithm has enjoyed broad adoption, the non-convexity of the associated energy has made it difficult to rigorously understand what the algorithm captures in many settings. In particular, a number of well-known numerical examples, several of which are reproduced in this article, suggest a complicated energy landscape with many local minimizers that do not respect the topology or clustering structure of the underlying data. This work seeks to provide first steps towards a rigorous explanation of these phenomena. Specifically, for a general family of energies, which include both the original t-SNE algorithm and recently identified large data limits, and for densities in feature space which obey a continuous symmetry, we construct infinite families of distinct critical points. These critical points are based upon identifying pairs of discrete symmetries, one in the original feature space and the other in the target embedding space, which are preserved under gradient dynamics. These critical configurations exhibit many characteristics, such as topology breaking and spurious clustering, which are often observed empirically. Finally, numerical and analytical examples are given throughout as a means of illustrating the approach.

</details>


### [231] [Disentangling Attention in Deep Operator Learning: A Controlled Study of Data-Driven and Physics-Informed Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04407)
*Amar Alem Koric,Qibang Liu,Seid Koric*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对五种不同注意力机制的DeepONet变体进行了系统对照研究，在数据驱动和物理信息两种训练范式下，通过三个偏微分方程基准问题隔离分析了交叉注意力、自注意力、token化和注意力深度的影响。结果显示，逐传感器token化结合交叉注意力可将经典DeepONet的相对L2误差降低2.4-28.0倍，最优配置可达3.5-32.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经算子能够以远快于传统数值求解器的速度学习输入函数到完整偏微分方程解场的映射，注意力机制已被引入神经算子中，但多数研究同时改变多个架构组件，难以辨识真正提升精度的因素。

Method: 对五种具有不同注意力机制的DeepONet变体进行受控系统性研究，分别在数据驱动和物理信息两种训练机制下训练，以隔离交叉注意力、自注意力、token化和注意力深度各自的影响。使用三个基准问题：一维非线性扩散-反应方程、一维粘性Burgers方程（可变初始条件）、二维Poisson热传导问题（异质源场）。

Result: 逐传感器token化加交叉注意力在所有基准-训练组合中均显著优于经典DeepONet，误差降低2.4-28.0倍，最优注意力配置达3.5-32.3倍。分支自注意力仅与点积融合时表现不一致（恶化一维问题但改善二维问题）；叠加在交叉注意力上可改善全部六个案例但增益较小。全局预混合无一致益处。增加交叉注意力深度可进一步提升精度但收益递减，物理信息训练下计算成本显著增加。

Conclusion: 查询依赖的交叉注意力是最可靠的注意力机制，而分支自注意力最适合大而空间复杂的函数输入。

Abstract: Deep neural operators learn mappings between input functions and complete PDE solution fields, enabling forward evaluations of new problem instances orders of magnitude faster than conventional numerical solvers. Attention mechanisms have recently been introduced into neural operators, but most studies change several architectural components at once, making it difficult to identify what actually improves accuracy. This work presents a controlled and systematic study of five deep operator network (DeepONet) variants with distinct attention mechanisms, trained under both data-driven and physics-informed regimes, to isolate the effects of cross-attention, self-attention, tokenization, and attention depth. We evaluate them on a source-driven transient one-dimensional nonlinear diffusion-reaction equation, a transient one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with variable initial conditions, and a two-dimensional Poisson heat-conduction problem with heterogeneous source fields. Per-sensor tokenization with cross-attention reduces the mean relative L_2 error of the classical DeepONet in all benchmark-training combinations by factors of 2.4-28.0, while the best attention configurations reach 3.5-32.3. Branch self-attention paired only with dot-product fusion is inconsistent, degrading the one-dimensional problems while helping the more complex two-dimensional source field; added on top of cross-attention it improves all six cases, though by less than cross-attention fusion alone. Global pre-mixing provides no consistent benefit. Increasing cross-attention depth further improves accuracy, but with diminishing returns and a substantially higher cost under physics-informed training. Overall, query-dependent cross-attention is the most reliable mechanism, whereas branch self-attention is most useful for large, spatially complex functional inputs.

</details>


### [232] [REFINE: LLM Refinement over Budgeted Text-Attributed Graphs for Personalized Medical Concept Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04415)
*Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Dongjie Wang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: REFINE是一个KG感知的预算受限LLM图细化框架，用于患者个性化医学概念编码。它通过强化学习策略为每个观察到的医学代码选择个性化的KG扩展预算，并结合异构GNN和冻结LLM的图感知软提示，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上显著提升了多种EHR预测骨干模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码器对患者采用统一的概念处理方式，忽略了医学代码的含义和预测价值依赖于患者特定的临床背景和病程轨迹这一事实。从文本属性知识图谱学习患者个性化概念表征面临两个关键挑战：如何决定每个观察代码应融入多少KG上下文，以及如何将语义信息与患者特定的关系结构对齐。

Method: REFINE框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从全局TKG构建患者特定的时间图；(2) 利用序列强化学习策略为每个观察到的代码选择个性化的KG扩展预算；(3) 通过异构GNN捕获关系感知的结构依赖，同时使用冻结的LLM结合图感知软提示对概念表征进行语义细化。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上的实验表明，REFINE一致性地提升了多种EHR骨干模型的性能，优于强基线方法，并在组件消融、KG选择和数据不足场景下展现出稳健的性能增益。

Conclusion: REFINE通过个性化KG扩展预算和LLM语义细化有效地解决了患者个性化医学概念编码问题，验证了结合强化学习、图神经网络和大型语言模型的可行性，为EHR预测提供了新的方法论参考。

Abstract: Learning rich medical concept representations is essential for EHR prediction. Text-attributed knowledge graphs (TKGs) provide a natural foundation by organizing heterogeneous medical relations together with textual semantics. However, most existing encoders process concepts uniformly across patients, despite the fact that a code's meaning and predictive value depend on patient-specific clinical context and trajectory. Learning patient-personalized concept representations from TKGs introduces two key challenges: (1) deciding how much KG context to incorporate for each observed code, and (2) aligning semantic information with the patient-specific relational structure. We propose REFINE, a KG-aware budgeted LLM graph refinement framework for patient-personalized medical concept encoding. Starting from a global TKG, REFINE constructs patient-specific temporal graphs. A sequential reinforcement learning policy selects a personalized KG expansion budget for each observed code. The resulting patient graph is processed by a heterogeneous GNN to capture relation-aware structural dependencies, while a frozen LLM uses graph-aware soft prompts to semantically refine concept representations. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV show that REFINE consistently improves diverse EHR backbones, outperforms strong baselines, and demonstrates robust gains across component ablation, KG selection, and data insufficiency.
